# 2.0 pre-calibration audit: consolidated ranked findings

Branch `2.0` at `f5660026`, 2026-09-21. Twenty-five read-only reviewers (one per Rust crate or crate section, plus the Python package, test suites, scripts/CI, docs, and evidence registries) each read their unit against three axes: mathematical accuracy, epistemic honesty, engineering quality.

**Status of every item below: candidate.** Reviewers could not build or run the repository (a baseline build held the lock); evidence is code reading plus standalone simulations. A finding is accepted only when a regression test reproduces it on the current code. Items marked *verified* were additionally checked by the coordinator against the source.

## Totals

427 findings: **12 critical**, **84 high**, **182 medium**, **149 low**. By axis: 214 honesty, 107 engineering, 105 math, 1 mixed. The 106 critical/high findings (and closely tied mediums) consolidate into **15 root-cause issues** (R01–R15), ranked below.

## Baseline on the unmodified tree

| Check | Result |
| --- | --- |
| `cargo fmt`, `cargo test --workspace` (24 min), 17 feature gates, all gate self-tests | pass |
| `cargo clippy --workspace --all-targets -- -D warnings` | **fail** — 66 warnings, ~15 files (learner/transport code, one bench) |
| `cargo doc` with `-D warnings` | **fail** — unresolved `NaturalDirect` link, `antecedent-core/src/query/mediation.rs:23` |
| `cargo deny check` | **fail** — RUSTSEC-2025-0141 (bincode unmaintained) via burn → `antecedent-learn-burn` |
| ruff | **fail** — import order in 2 test files |
| pytest (2,125 tests, 90% coverage) | **2 fail** — `provider`, `controls` rows missing from the public-products registry |
| CodeQL | **4 Python findings** in `python/antecedent/transport/` (unused import; three `x != x` NaN checks); Rust 0, Actions 0 |
| `gate_release.sh` | stops at calibration attestation: all 591 coverage records owe re-measurement (expected) |

The existing test suite is green with every defect below present, so each fix needs a new adversarial fixture, not only a code change. A full calibration re-measurement is estimated at 15–30 hours; every change to the statistical surface or the calibration harness (R13) must land before it starts.

### R01

**Identification reports a causal claim it has not earned** — critical — **closed**

Twelve findings are **closed**. `estimate-structural-5` is **scoped**: the "intervened" predictor could not be identified from a graph it never receives, so it was relabelled to what it actually computes rather than claiming `do()`. One check is **refuted**: Python already reads the facade-narrowed per-estimand claim, not an independent union.

*Root cause.* PAG/MAG response queries fall back to Shpitser–Pearl ID on the MAG read as an ADMG, so invisible edges are treated as unconfounded and the effect is reported identified; sharp RD reports a population ATE from config alone; the linear front-door estimate is attached to a nonparametric `Identified` status; Auto overwrites (rather than unions) required assumptions; ID is documented complete but errors on the napkin graph; PAG m-separation certifies independence across circle–circle non-colliders.

*Root fix.* Remove the MAG-as-ADMG fallback (refuse when generalized adjustment refuses, unless visibility is certified); scope RD to the local effect at the cutoff and front-door to a recorded linearity assumption; union assumptions in Auto; implement ID line-7→2→6/7 ratio conditionals and add a napkin fixture, or scope the completeness claim; fix definite-status path handling in `pag.rs`.

*Moves published numbers:* `identify-a-1`, `docs-claims-3`.

*Disposition (2026-09-21, branch `2.0`).* Every R01 claim was reproduced by a test that failed on `f5660026`; none was refuted. The preferred repair was to earn the claim: ID is now complete on the ADMG it is given (evidence, not proof), MAG/PAG response identification is sound beyond adjustment, the front-door functional has its own estimator, and sharp RD carries a typed local estimand. One claim could not be earned and was relabelled (`estimate-structural-5`). Landed as 19 commits, `ccdc10ca`..`1ee39dc8` after rebase onto R02. Verified with the touched crates' tests, a full facade run per unit, and the registry gates on the rebased tree; no full-workspace run on the final tree.

| Status | Finding | Disposition |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **closed** | `identify-a-1`, `docs-claims-3` | Reproduced: a two-node MAG with an invisible `T -> Y` came back identified with `P(y\|t)`. ID now runs on `mag_to_confounded_admg`, which adds `A <-> B` beside every invisible `A -> B`. Soundness was established by enumeration, not assumed: for every DAG with ≤4 observed + ≤2 latent binary nodes (61,450) and 3,000 sampled 5-observed DAGs the latent projection is an edge-subgraph of that ADMG, and every identified `E[Y\|do(T)]` matched exact SCM truth (71,712 checks, worst error 8e-16; the old reading is numerically wrong 5,815 times, so the test can fail). Earned beyond adjustment on a five-node MAG with no adjustment set. Every route validates the MAG; the invalid pinned front-door "MAG" is now refused. Sound, not complete; IDP cited as the complete algorithm. |
| **closed** | `data-graph-1` | Reproduced: `X o-o B o-o Y` was certified separated. Unshielded non-collider triples are definite; indefinite-status paths are no longer discarded. `Pag::m_separation_status` returns `Separated` / `Connected` / `Undetermined`, and `is_m_separated` errors instead of returning `true` when undetermined. Agrees exactly with brute force over member MAGs (2,637 PAGs, 80,312 queries), which also exposed a second bug (a collider opened only by a descendant reached over a circle mark). |
| **closed** | `identify-a-2` | Reproduced: napkin returned `Err`. The working distribution is now a recursive law; conditionals of a marginalized law are emitted as `Ratio{N, SumOut{v, N}}`. Exhaustive sweep of every ADMG on 2–4 nodes × every ordered (treatment, outcome) pair: 49,544 queries, 40,381 identified and equal to enumerated truth to 1e-9, 9,163 refuted by a hedge that `HedgeCertificate::verify` checks against the original query, zero errors; plus 1,500 sampled 5–6-node joint queries and a doubly nested napkin. ID/IDC now call `validate()` and refuse `Y ∩ X ≠ ∅`. IDC was swept and found sound (no change). |
| **closed** | `identify-a-3` | Reproduced. Auto does not choose a strategy — the facade does when it picks an estimand — so each estimand now carries its own claim (`estimand_claims`, `narrowed_to`, facade `select_claim`). One item was explicitly deferred: `mediation.linear` has the same claim-mismatch pattern the front-door fix addressed (reports nonparametric status while `total - direct` is not the certified natural indirect effect under interaction) — tracked separately below. |

### R02

**Published uncertainty ignores what the estimator licensed** — critical — **closed**

Ten findings are **closed**. `facade-top-pyo3-3` is **scoped**: a scalar standard error is still a labeled normal interval. Not open R02 findings: the Abadie–Imbens 2·ΣK term (R06); HC1/cluster/multiway Anderson–Rubin, which is withheld rather than estimated; the calibration grid has no `anderson_rubin` record yet (`estimator_grid_not_measured`).

*Root cause.* The facade publishes `se_bootstrap.or_else(se_analytic)` and pushes its default 199 replicates into every estimator. The IV weak-instrument gate only NaNs the analytic SE, so a nominal 95% interval is still published at F<10; matching's bootstrap, which its own module documents as invalid, becomes the primary interval; the rule is copied in four places with two predicates; support status has no estimator axis, so any estimator inside a licensed cell reports `licensed`.

*Root fix.* Each estimator declares its licensed and withheld uncertainty products with reasons; one `published_uncertainty()` selector feeds the wire SE, interval label, Uncertainty slot and both Python copies; add an estimator axis to support licensing; surface first-stage diagnostics; replace the F≥10 pretest with weak-instrument-robust inference or an honest refusal.

*Moves published numbers:* `facade-execute-1`, `facade-execute-2`, `facade-analysis-1`, `estimate-structural-1`, `estimate-structural-2`, `estimate-static-3`, `facade-top-pyo3-2`, `facade-top-pyo3-3`, `facade-analysis-4`.

### R03

**FCI-family orientation rules deviate from Zhang (2008)** — critical — **closed**

All six findings are **closed**. R3 requires the circle at θ, R8 requires definite `b → c`, and R10 requires definite parents plus non-adjacent first nodes. Ambiguous unshielded triples are a set on `OrientationState` that Meek R1 skips. PCMCI+ majority votes return CI and MCI-truncation errors.

*Root cause.* R3 tests circle marks at `a` and `c` instead of at θ (verified by reading the code) and two tests pin the inverted premise; R8′ accepts `a → b o→ c` and does not require an arrowhead at `c`; R10′ omits the non-adjacency premise. Shared by FCI, RFCI and LPCMCI. Unknown-status triples are oriented as definite non-colliders; PCMCI+ majority voting swallows CI errors; GES's sink test ignores directed parents.

*Root fix.* Re-derive R3/R8/R10 line by line from Zhang (2008) and the LPCMCI variant, with oracle-graph fixtures for each rule; carry an explicit ambiguous-triple state; make CI errors refuse; fix the Dor–Tarsi test.

*Moves published numbers:* `discovery-1`, `discovery-2`, `discovery-3`, `discovery-4`, `discovery-6`.

### R04

**Bayesian prior and posterior scale errors** — critical — **closed**

All seven findings are **closed**. Coefficient `variance` is conjugate scale `V0` (`β | σ² ~ N(μ, σ² diag(V0))`). Absolute SD² is converted with `from_absolute_variance` using the source residual-variance mean. When that column is absent, hydration uses `σ² = 1`, which is the absolute scale only if the residual variance really is 1.

*Root cause.* `GaussianCoefficientPrior.variance` is σ²-relative for Gaussian likelihoods but hydrated/transferred priors write an absolute SD² into it (found independently by two reviewers); the NIG scale is computed as `y'y − mₙ'Vₙ⁻¹mₙ`, which cancels catastrophically on uncentred designs; prior transport writes the heterogeneity variance of unit effects into the coefficient prior variance with a 1e-12 floor; diagonal hydration makes sequential posteriors too narrow.

*Root fix.* Make the variance slot's scale explicit in the type (absolute vs σ²-relative) and convert at every boundary; compute `beta_n` from RSS plus the prior quadratic form; carry the source posterior variance through transport; hydrate the joint covariance or disclose the diagonal approximation; add closed-form conjugate tests at σ∈{0.1,10} with a non-zero prior mean and a pooled-equals-sequential test.

*Moves published numbers:* `prob-1`, `prob-2`, `prob-3`, `estimate-bayes-transport-response-1`, `estimate-bayes-transport-response-2`, `docs-claims-4`.

### R05

**Conditional-independence tests are miscalibrated or fail open on bad input** — critical

*Root cause.* kNN conditional test has Type-I ≈ 1.0 with continuous Z; G² has no sparse-table guard (Type-I 0.41–0.94); weighted partial correlation ignores the weights in its df; GPDC uses a fixed length scale on unstandardised Z; NaN input yields a finite statistic (parcorr returns r=0 with non-empty Z; ranks/categories coerce NaN) that discovery's only `is_finite` check accepts.

*Root fix.* Permute within local neighbourhoods of Z (or refuse continuous Z); add a sparse-table df/expected-count guard with disclosure; use Kish effective n; standardise inputs for GPDC; refuse non-finite input at the kernel boundary; calibrate every test with non-empty, non-unit-scale Z.

*Moves published numbers:* `stats-ci-1`, `stats-ci-2`, `stats-ci-3`, `stats-ci-4`.

| `stats-ci-2` | high | gsquared.rs:176-202, 204-270, 274-282 | G² chi-square reference is used with no sparse-table guard; Type I reaches 0.4–0.94 at ordinary PC conditioning depths | confirmed (port + scipy cross-check) |

### R06

**Standard errors that are simply wrong** — critical

*Root cause.* Matching ATE uses the Abadie–Imbens ATT form and drops the 2ΣK cross term (simulated 95% coverage 0.889); matching's cluster/HAC pseudo-influence is not the estimator's influence function; causal-forest SE divides by √B as if trees were independent and the forest is labelled GRF-style without local centering; negative-binomial g-computation SE ignores α; the temporal-response level influence adds an O(1/n) residual term to an O(1) term, mis-scaling the kernel-bias factor and short-series warning for every temporal band; indefinite multiway meat is clamped to SE=0.

*Root fix.* Correct each formula against its source with a simulation-backed coverage test; rename or re-implement the forest honestly; refuse (not clamp) a non-PSD multiway meat.

*Moves published numbers:* `estimate-static-1`, `estimate-static-2`, `estimate-static-4`, `estimate-static-5`, `estimate-static-6`, `estimate-temporal-1`.

### R07

**Python transport surface authors scientific status itself** — critical

*Root cause.* Front-door Transport results get a Python-invented `se_analytic = 0.0`; `export()` writes an unauthenticated JSON blob and `load()` trusts its status; a catalog-search exception becomes `identified` with Support available (verified); identification view, validation verdict and failure reasons are defaulted or relabelled in Python; binding-side inspections are hand-typed JSON with a hard-coded identified status.

*Root fix.* Python reads native fields only; route export/load through the Rust consumer; delete Python-side defaults for status, validation and reasons; generate the four-slot inspection from the native result.

### R08

**Attribution methods that do not measure what they claim** — critical

*Root cause.* Path-based change attribution never uses the baseline/comparison fits (on the crate's own fixture it attributes +3.33 to X when the truth is 0); mechanism-change tests compare in-sample with out-of-sample residuals (Type-I up to 61% under covariate shift); a 1-row or constant segment returns p=0; the nonlinear robust path evaluates f(E[X]) instead of E[f(X)].

*Root fix.* Re-implement path-based allocation from the two fits; use out-of-sample residuals on both sides; add minimum-segment gates in `gaussian_segment_lr` and its callers; integrate over root noise in the nonlinear path.

*Moves published numbers:* `attr-design-state-1`, `attr-design-state-2`, `attr-design-state-6`, `stats-a-3`.

### R09

**Refusals, errors and search budgets rendered as results** — high

*Root cause.* Search-budget exits reported as proven non-identification; cancellation and resource exhaustion flattened into scientific error kinds; `Constant` mechanism selected when every other family fails (zeroing do-effects); density failure swallowed and labelled IPW; zero/NaN SE gives p=0 and a BH discovery; max-t failure silently drops the simultaneous interval; failed fits counted as unidentified mass; edgeless graph emitted on orientation failure; discovery silently runs on unit 0 of a panel.

*Root fix.* Keep refusal / unsupported / numerical / cancelled / budget distinct end to end; never substitute a value for a failure; every swallow becomes a typed error or an explicit diagnostic.

### R10

**NaN used as the symbolic-intervention sentinel** — high

*Root cause.* `Value::Float64(NaN)` marks a symbolic intervention, so symbolic sets are never hash-consed (NaN≠NaN), a genuine NaN intervention aliases the wildcard and turns E[Y|do(T=NaN)] into a sum over T, and ratio-form conditionals bypass the `sampling_zero` guard.

*Root fix.* Add an explicit `Symbolic` variant to `InterventionAssignment` (the wire form already carries `symbolic: bool`); validate intervention finiteness in core; apply the support guard to ratio-form conditionals.

*Moves published numbers:* `expr-3`.

### R11

**Seed streams are additive in the stream id** — high

*Root cause.* `mix_seed(master + stream_id)` makes (s,k) ≡ (s−δ,k+δ): adjacent master seeds share 999 of 1000 bootstrap replicates, so a seed-stability check cannot fail; several `stream(CONST + index)` families overlap.

*Root fix.* Domain-separated `stream_for(StreamDomain, index)` with a closed enum and a non-additive mixer; migrate all 108 `.stream(` sites. Moves every seeded output.

*Moves published numbers:* `data-graph-3`, `core-kernels-2`, `docs-claims-13`.

### R12

**Refuters and sensitivity checks that cannot fail** — high

*Root cause.* Placebo, dummy-outcome, random-common-cause and data-subset refuters cannot fail for OLS yet report `informative: true` as falsifiers; the robustness value is an upper bound combined with a `>=` pass rule; NaN replicates give p=1.0 (pass); `falsified` is an uncorrected union of α-level tests.

*Root fix.* Report non-informative refuters as such (or redesign them so they have power); fix the robustness-value definition and pass rule; NaN replicates fail closed; correct for multiplicity.

*Moves published numbers:* `validate-1`, `model-learn-cf-4`.

| `validate-2` | high | `common.rs:685-769` (`noise_replace_refute`, `replicate_p_value`), `placebo.rs:90-153`, `rcc.rs:50-118`, `data_subset.rs:55-123`, `mediation.rs:135-180,228-241` | Placebo, dummy-outcome, RCC and data-subset refuters cannot fail for the estimator they are gated to; reported `informative: true` and grouped as "Falsifiers of the causal claim" | confirmed |

| `registries-3` | high | parity/oracle_closure.toml:727-737 (`validate.confounding_sensitivity`, oracle "sensemakr and DoWhy", pin `sensemakr==0.1.4, dowhy==0.14`, comparison "numeric", | `oracle_closure.toml` records external-package numeric validation for fixtures that contain no output of that package | confirmed |

### R13

**Calibration harness and evidence that cannot catch defects** — high

*Root cause.* Recheck/floor only on the low side; reported-level (0.95) records never rechecked; DML/DR/forest have no coverage record; failed fits leave the denominator; three calibration tests are orphans (one scores the confounded contrast 3.0 as truth where the causal ATE is 2.0); every coverage DGP sits inside the estimator's assumptions; conformance tolerances admit the unadjusted estimate and an 8× SE error; 78 licensed cells rest on tests that only assert completion; runtime `calibrated` ignores record freshness. Must land BEFORE the 15–30 h calibration run.

*Root fix.* Symmetric recheck and ceiling, applied to reported-level records; enrol DML/DR/forest; skips count as misses with a 1% cap; wire or delete orphans and fix the wrong truth; add adversarial cells (weak IV, weak overlap, curved RD, heterogeneous matching, uncentred/rescaled Bayesian); tighten tolerances; bind runtime calibration status to attestation.

*Moves published numbers:* `scripts-ci-2`, `tests-quality-3`, `tests-quality-9`, `tests-quality-10`.

### R14

**Graph interchange silently changes the graph; untrusted files can crash or exhaust memory** — high

*Root cause.* Standard GML and real NetworkX adjacency documents load as edgeless graphs; DOT re-import drops edge attributes so a bidirected edge becomes a causal edge; unnamed DOT export drops isolated nodes and renumbers variables; zstd decompression is unbounded; truncated mapped files panic; result-artifact decode leaves whole axes unvalidated and accepts an estimate under `not_identified`.

*Root fix.* Round-trip property tests for every format and graph class; refuse unknown/unsupported attributes instead of ignoring them; bounded streaming decompression; bounds checks on every mapped slice; complete `validate_result`.

### R15

**Other correctness defects** — high

*Root cause.* Local-quadratic singularity test is unit-dependent (doses with sd≈1000 refused as discrete); CPDAG completion is a blind 2^k scan (hangs at ≥25 undirected edges); likelihood-weighted conditional-do sampler does not condition descendants; `unit_rows` unchecked (panic through the facade); graph-entropy design channel scores a no-op as informative; random-intercept whitening applied regardless of likelihood (facade reviewer says the builder refuses upstream — needs one check); LiNGAM has no non-Gaussianity check; discovery results ship an empty `AssumptionSet`; `publish_crates.sh` omits `antecedent-learn`.

*Root fix.* Individually, each with a failing test first.

## Leads checked and refuted, and algorithms reviewed and found sound

These matter as much as the findings: they record what was examined and found correct.

### attr-design-state — refuted leads

- **`shapley.rs:157-166` catch-all `_ => return Ok(())` skips the budget check.** `ShapleyMode` has exactly three variants (the match in `core/query/attribution.rs::validate` is exhaustive without a wildcard), so the arm is unreachable today; both callers go on to `estimate_shapley`, which does reject unknown variants (`:322`). Residual risk only: a future variant would pass the budget guard — prefer `Err(unsupported)` here too. Not a current defect.
- **`shapley.rs:423` doc claims parity with `ranker.rs:452`.** The doc claims only the `n < 2 → +∞` convention, which matches. The cancellation problem is real but lives in the ranker (finding 8).
- **Permutation-sampling unbiasedness.** Uniform Fisher–Yates permutations, per-permutation marginal chain, running mean: unbiased; per-permutation efficiency holds exactly so Σφ̂ = v(N) − v(∅) in MC mode too (tested at `:513`). No antithetic sampling (not claimed). `MonteCarlo` and `Permutation` are the same algorithm; `ShapleyConfig::monte_carlo` doc ("n_samples coalition evaluations") is loose — it is n permutations × (k+1) evaluations.
- **`:60` t multiplier.** `student_t_ppf(0.975, n−1)` with `n = completed` permutations is right for the permutation-mean; n = 1 → ∞·∞ = ∞ as documented. (What the interval *covers* is finding 10.)
- **Exact Shapley weights / efficiency.** `s!(n−s−1)!/n!` correct; loop adds each (S, i∉S) marginal once; efficiency tested on an interactive game (`:748`).
- **`structure_change.rs:103` `expect("player in layout")`.** Players are constructed from `baseline.output_layout.variables[i]`, so the lookup cannot fail. Only an O(n) scan.
- **`feature_relevance.rs:103` "user seed used directly as stream id".** The seed is the constant `0xFEA7`, not user input; it is deliberately fixed for CRN across coalitions. (Whether the stream stays aligned when an intervened node skips its draw depends on `sample_interventional`, outside this unit.)
- **`preposterior.rs:33` "unbiased for EVSI" / nested-MC bias.** Not nested: the prior *is* the finite draw set, the posterior over draws is computed exactly by Bayes' rule for each simulated statistic, and `max_a` is applied to an exact inner expectation. The outer mean over (θ, y) is therefore unbiased for EVSI **under the K-draw empirical prior**. The doc should say "with respect to the empirical prior over the supplied draws" (finite-K bias relative to the underlying continuous belief remains), but the claim as implemented holds. Exact-enumeration path: `Σ_y (1/K) max_a Σ_k L_k(y)·gap_ak` with a mass check to 1e-6 — correct. Conjugate path: `s² = τ²·nτ²/(nτ²+σ²)` correct; `expected_max_affine` upper-envelope construction and the segment integral `c·P + d·(φ(l) − φ(r))` verified by hand and by the two unit tests (E|Z| = √(2/π)).
- **`graph_score.rs:193` `sorted_parents` duplicate of `structure_change.rs:189`.** Same name, different types and semantics (u32 slice with dedup + self-parent check vs `Dag` parents by `DenseNodeId`); not shared logic.
- **`ranker.rs:643,676` `/total.max(1e-15)`.** `WeightedGraphSamples::new` validates weights and `validate_rank_inputs` rejects an empty posterior; the floor is unreachable defensive code, not a substitution on a live path.
- **Particle-filter weights / resampling.** Log-weights normalised by max-subtraction (log-sum-exp-safe), errors on all-`−∞`; ESS = 1/Σw² with threshold N/2; systematic resampling with a single u₀ ∈ [0,1/N) and a correct cumulative walk with end-guard; log-weights reset to 0 after resampling; failed steps are atomic (scratch-then-swap, RNG state committed last). No log-likelihood estimate is produced, so there is nothing to mis-state. Initial law is N(0, σ_proc²) rather than the stationary N(0, σ²/(1−a²)) — documented on `init`.
- **Incremental = batch for sufficient statistics.** `StreamingCovariance::append` is the standard rank-one Welford update with the n/(n+1) factor and old means on both axes (symmetric by construction); `append_batch` is a loop over `append_row`, so equality is structural. Rolling window: raw-Gram downdate with a full rebuild every 64 appends, and window σ² taken from recomputed residuals rather than the downdated scatter — sound.

### attr-design-state — reviewed and sound

- `shapley.rs`: exact enumeration, factorial weights, MC/permutation estimator with Welford per-player variance, `sequential_allocate` marginals and the consecutive-pair interaction residual `v(S∪ab) − v(S∪a) − v(S∪b) + v(S)`, size guards (k ≤ 64, exact k ≤ 63, `1u128 << k.min(64)`).
- `coalition.rs`: dense/HashMap cache, byte accounting, sticky saturation, `full_coalition_mask` at the 64-bit boundary.
- `change_common.rs`: KL payoff with v(∅) ≡ 0 and cached baseline law; `path_efficiency_scale` refusal logic (the *use* of it is finding 1).
- `distribution_change.rs`: player construction (every node has a gather, so roots are mechanism players and `PlayerKind::Input` is genuinely unreachable), CRN per coalition (same stream ⇒ deterministic v(S), so a disabled cache cannot break efficiency).
- `anomaly.rs::model_implied_variances`: full-covariance propagation in topological order (`Cov(j,k) = Σ_p β_p Cov(p,k)`, `Var(j) = σ_j² + ΣΣ β_pβ_q Cov(p,q)`) — correct, including shared ancestors; arrow strength β²Var(pa) is documented as the first-order approximation of Janzing et al.'s −½log(1−·).
- `anomaly.rs::OutlierTail`: median/1.4826·MAD, asymptotic log-tail switch past `norm_sf` underflow (`½z² + ln z + ½ln 2π − ln 2`) — correct expansion of −ln(2φ(z)/z).
- `root_cause.rs::ContributionMoments`: West's weighted incremental mean/variance; union-of-components zero-fill; weight rescaling by max.
- `mechanism_change.rs`: raw vs BH-adjusted p-value reporting and the `changed` decision; per-(target, population) RNG streams.
- `decision.rs`: admissible-set construction, Bayes action, EVPI as mean of non-negative gaps (origin-invariant).
- `preposterior.rs`: all three evaluation paths (see refuted lead), `BinomialSignal` log-mass incl. θ ∈ {0,1} edge cases, `finite_max` NaN/+∞ rejection.
- `particle_filter.rs`, `suff_stats.rs::StreamingCovariance`, `mechanism_diag.rs` rolling window, `state.rs` apply/invalidate/publish atomicity (version bump only after validation; staged batch commit), `graph_score.rs` Gaussian BIC formula (`−n/2(1+ln2π+ln σ̂²) − k/2·ln n`, σ̂² = RSS/n; the omitted variance parameter is constant across parent sets).

### calibration-harness — checked and sound

- A non-finite or inverted interval counts as a miss, never a skip (calibration.rs:630-636).
- Band, recheck and floor apply per grid point. A failing point is never averaged into a pass (gate_calibration.sh:81-101).
- Records are bound to the facade-reported construction, not hand-declared.
- Smoke runs cannot enter the registry.
- There are no `|rate − nominal| < 0.04`-style overrides.
- Registry aggregates:
  - For non-boundary gated points, mean (observed − nominal) is +0.0012 over 642 points at level 0.90 and −0.0010 over 398 points at level 0.95. There is no systemic under-coverage.
  - Replicate counts are 400 for 1242 points, 2000 for 420 and 1000 for 90.
- `crates/antecedent-estimate/tests/coverage_determinism.rs` is designed to test bit-reproducibility and per-grid-point data in ordinary CI. I read only its doc comment.
- Attestation:
  - CI's attestation gate checks the facets against each record's `calibration_sha`.
  - 587 of 591 records are stamped at 6a41568f, 54 commits behind HEAD.
  - The 11 replay waivers say their evidence is a bit-for-bit replay.
- Boundary cells state the mechanism and the measured SE/SD ratio, and those records are not labelled `calibrated`.
- The gate runs each group once per grid point, at n/2, n and 2n, with independently salted data.
- Four test files were only skimmed for what they claim to do: `calibration_binding.rs`, `calibration_grid_binding.rs`, `production_replicates.rs` and `v110_panel_calibration.rs`.

### core-kernels — refuted leads

- `execution.rs:562 slot.expect("every index was filled")` panicking on cancellation: refuted. Workers have no early-exit path (no cancellation check, no `break`); each writes every slot of its chunk. If `f` panics, `std::thread::scope` re-panics before the `expect` is reached. Chunks are contiguous `split_at_mut` slices, results are returned in index order, and `collect::<Result<_,_>>` yields the lowest-index error — deterministic irrespective of thread count (streams are keyed by index, and the inner ctx shares the same `RngFactory`).
- `query/attribution.rs:370` NaN -> 0 bits: benign. 0 bits == 0.0, which `MechanismChangeQuery::validate` rejects (`alpha > 0.0 && alpha < 1.0`), so a NaN level cannot pass validation or alias a valid level.
- `special.rs` erf/erfc/norm_cdf accuracy: refuted. Python port of the exact coefficients vs `math.erf/erfc` on a 0.0037 grid over [-10, 26.5]: max relative error 2.2e-16 (erfc) and 2.2e-16 (erf, [-6,6]). Coefficients match Cody CALERF; thresholds 0.46875 / 4 / 27.3 correct; NaN propagates (all comparisons false -> asymptotic branch -> NaN), +/-inf give 0 / 2. `norm_cdf`/`norm_sf` via erfc keep relative tail accuracy. `norm_inv` is plain Acklam (~1.15e-9 relative, no Halley refinement) and documented as such.
- `scalar.rs` `get_unchecked`: sound. Every use is inside `for i in 0..x.len()`; `F64VectorView::strided` validates `(len-1)*stride+1 <= data.len()` with overflow checks; `contiguous` sets len = data.len(). `gather`'s `expect` is a documented panic on an internal index contract.
- `standardize_inplace` n vs n-1: uses n-1 for n > 1, scale = max(1, eps) for n = 1, empty -> no mutation; matches its doc. `masked_variance`/`masked_covariance` are documented population (÷n) forms; `PosteriorReduceOp::Std` correctly rescales by n/(n-1).
- Identity format: `IDENTITY_FORMAT = 2` while derive keys end in `.v1` is fine — antecedent-io prefixes the format u16 into every hash (`antecedent-io/src/identity.rs:65-66`).
- Placebo stream overlap (`0xA7E0_0001_0000` vs `0xA7E0_0001_1000 + r`): the two modes are mutually exclusive branches.

### core-kernels — reviewed and sound

- SplitMix64 `next_u64` / `next_f64` (constants, shifts, 53-bit mantissa), `from_seed` xor.
- `unbiased_index` rejection bound (`limit` is a multiple of n) and Fisher–Yates `shuffle`.
- Cody erf/erfc, `exp_neg_square_split`, `norm_cdf`, `norm_sf`, `norm_pdf`, Acklam `norm_inv` branches and endpoints.
- `parcorr.rs` normal-equation residualization: centering == intercept, Gauss–Jordan with partial pivoting and relative singularity tolerance, ridge retry, scalar vs portable paths algebraically identical (differ only in summation order).
- `AdaptiveBootstrapBudget::required_replicates` (ceil(1 + 1/(2 eps^2)) = 201 at 0.05, NaN/<=0 -> never stop, saturating cast).
- `EvidenceRegime::try_new` / `project` (de-intervention refused, marginalize/condition rules), exact-set semantics of `available_experiment_on` (do(A), do(B) never satisfy do(A,B)), `canonicalized` ordering, `TransportQuery::validate`/`with_catalog` agreement check.
- `claim_compatibility` parent-count rules, Pool always refused, retarget product/premise equality, domain-upgrade refusal; `AcceptanceReport` storage-vs-claim distinction.
- `TransformationReport::compose` obligation merge (unresolved wins, later discharge cannot erase) and refusal propagation.
- `TargetPopulation::resolve` (binary-treatment check, row bounds, weight finiteness), `StochasticPolicy::validate`.

### data-graph — refuted leads

- **d-separation correctness (`dsep.rs`, incl. `:312` expect).** Ancestral-moral-graph algorithm is implemented correctly: ancestral closure honours the overlay, moral edges only marry parents whose edges are visible and ancestral, conditioning nodes are skipped in the search, and descendants-of-colliders are handled by construction (a collider is in the ancestral set iff it or a descendant is in Z∪{x,y}). Verified against the file's own independent path-enumeration oracle (dsep_tests.rs:83-300, random DAGs n ≤ 5, all Z). The `expect("path predecessor")` at :312 is unreachable: `reconstruct_path` is only called after `d_sep_connected(.., record_path = true)` returned true, which sets `pred` for every visited node except `x`.
- **X∩Z ≠ ∅.** All three graph classes consistently return "not separated" / a two-node `Connected` witness when an endpoint is in Z, and document it (dsep.rs:90-91). Conservative for identification; not a bug. (The synthetic two-node "active_path" is not an actual path — cosmetic.)
- **`admg.rs:232,260,288` districts in an induced subgraph.** `Admg::districts()` is whole-graph by contract and its only external consumers (`identify/prepared.rs:49`, `transport.rs` `district_count == node_count`) want the whole graph. m-separation computes its own ancestral-restricted districts (msep.rs:212-232) correctly. No caller passes a subset expecting induced c-components from this API.
- **Treatment mutilation and bidirected edges.** `GraphOverlay`/`mutilate` exist only for `Dag` (no bidirected edges). ADMG mutilation is done in `antecedent-identify` (outside this unit).
- **`dag.rs:177` topological order determinism.** Kahn with a LIFO stack over `Vec` adjacency: deterministic for a given node/edge insertion order, no hash iteration. It *does* depend on edge insertion order (two equal graphs built in different edge order give different, both valid, orders); callers needing canonical output should sort — noted, not a defect here. `dag.rs:204` expect is guarded by `add_node`'s u32 check.
- **Latent projection (`projection.rs:56-82`).** Directed: BFS from u that only continues through latent nodes — correct for paths through any number of latents. Bidirected: ∃ latent L with latent-only directed paths to both endpoints ⇔ ∃ divergent path with all interior nodes latent (take the last common node) — correct, including multi-latent chains. The `Cycle` arms are dead (a projection of a DAG cannot cycle) and fail closed.
- **Arrow FFI ownership / offset / nulls / dtypes.** All raw CDI handling is delegated to `arrow::ffi::from_ffi` (release exactly once, offset applied, alignment enforced by `ScalarBuffer`). `ArrowCColumn` fields are private and `from_ffi` is `unsafe`, so safe code cannot build a mismatched pair. `floats.values()` is already offset-sliced; `FixedSizeListArray::values()` is offset-sliced by arrow-rs so `row*dim` indexing is right. Nulls → NaN + cleared validity on every path (`Float64Column::new` enforces invalid ⇔ NaN); borrow happens only when null slots already hold NaN. Non-f64 dtypes are rejected with `TypeMismatch`, never cast. `unsafe impl Send/Sync for ForeignF64Buffer` is justified: read-only pointer kept alive by an `Arc<dyn Send+Sync>` owner (`ArrayRef`).
- **Missing data flowing silently into estimators.** Default is listwise deletion (`MissingPolicy::CompleteCase`) but it is reported: `PreparedSample.dropped = DropSummary { requested, retained }` (sample_request.rs:352) and an all-dropped sample is an error. Lagged temporal paths refuse masked/incomplete series (`ensure_unmasked`, `ensure_complete_float`). Whether each estimator surfaces `dropped` is outside this unit.
- **Bootstrap correctness / thread-count determinism.** IID draws use rejection-sampled `unbiased_index` (no modulo bias); Fisher–Yates is correct; moving/circular block start ranges are right (`n−ℓ+1` vs `n`); stationary bootstrap geometric length has P(L=k)=p(1−p)^{k−1}; cluster bootstrap draws G whole clusters with replacement and correctly refuses fixed-width output for unequal cluster sizes; Bayesian bootstrap is normalised Exp(1) scaled to sum n. Per-replicate streams make the batch independent of thread count and chunking. (Stream *derivation* is data-graph-3.)
- **`transforms.rs` standardisation / constant column.** No standardisation lives in this crate. `equal_width_bin` guards zero range with `max(1e-12)` and clamps; `require_finite` rejects NaN/inf rather than mapping them.
- **`dataset.rs`/`storage.rs` O(n·k) copies on select.** Columns are `Arc<[OwnedColumn]>` with `Arc`-backed buffers; `with_analysis_mask`/`with_replaced_floats` clone `Arc`s, not cell data (`columns().to_vec()` clones column headers only). The real copy cost is the content digest recomputed on each `try_new` (full BLAKE3 pass over all cells per derived dataset) — acceptable, but worth knowing on refute loops.
- **CPDAG "Meek closure & validity checks", PAG "visible edges / possibly-directed paths".** Not implemented in `antecedent-graph` at all (the types are mark containers with cycle checks only); that logic lives in `antecedent-discovery` / `antecedent-identify`. Nothing to refute here, but note `Cpdag`/`Pag` accept structurally invalid members of their class (e.g. a `Pag` with an almost-directed cycle is accepted at insert; only `completion.rs` rejects it later).
- **`selection.rs` `try_new`.** Validates existence and uniqueness of targets; S-nodes are extensional so they cannot enter adjustment sets. Fine. (O(n) `contains` per target is immaterial.)

- `Dag::d_sep_connected` (ancestral moral graph, overlay-aware) — line by line.
- `Admg::moralize_ancestral_set` — Richardson augmentation: cliquing `C ∪ pa(C)` per ancestral-restricted district is exactly collider-connectedness (proved both directions in review); `Admg::markov_blanket` = neighbours in the full augmented graph is a valid (not necessarily minimal) blanket.
- `GraphOverlay` / `DagView` / `Dag::mutilate` — visibility predicate and materialisation agree (also property-tested).

- `latent_project` (see above).
- `is_maximal_ancestral_graph` — uses the Richardson–Spirtes `An({x,y})\{x,y}` separating set; exact for directed/bidirected MAGs. `is_ancestral_orientation` almost-directed-cycle test correct.
- `CompletionSampler` honesty logic: exhaustive over ≤16 sites, refuses above; ambiguous global class ⇒ yields nothing; skipped audit and cap are both surfaced and downgrade the claim in `generalized.rs`.
- `is_mec_member` (skeleton + unshielded colliders + compelled edges).
- `TemporalDag`/`TemporalPag` unfold shift ranges (`lo = min_off − min(offsets)`, `hi = max_off − max(offsets)`), `refine` conflict rejection.
- `TieredBackground::tier_closure_set`, `to_admg`, `to_pag` as graph constructions. **Cross-unit lead for the identify reviewer:** for a treatment *set* spanning tiers, the closure includes later-tier descendants of earlier treatments (mediators of T1 when T2 sits in a later tier); validity then rests entirely on `joint_adjustment_holds` rejecting such sets (identify/tiered.rs:170-173) — check that it tests the forbidden-descendant condition and not only proper-backdoor m-separation.
- `Float64Column::new` invariant (invalid ⇔ NaN) including the fused scan, `ValidityBitmap::{is_all_valid, gather, compact, concat}`.
- `compile_contrast_matrix` Treatment / SumToZero / Helmert / FullRank / Custom (k = 1 degenerates to a 0-column matrix, no underflow panic).
- `fill_block_slice`, `fill_stationary`, `fill_cluster_bootstrap`, `fill_within_cluster_permutation`, `fill_resample_weights`, `integer_cube_root_ceil`, `circular_block_length`.
- `apply_row_map` / `apply_weight_plan` (validity, analysis mask and weights follow the row map).
- `pool_multi_env_lagged_frame` — environments are lagged independently before stacking (no cross-environment lag windows); one-hot reference-level convention consistent.
- `DiscoveryEstimationSplit` constructors and range validation.

### discovery — refuted leads

- **`orientation.rs:993` sepset lookup** — keyed on the unordered non-adjacent pair (a,b) and tests membership of the centre c; correct. The stale-legs guard (`classify_collider_leg`) is correct.
- **Meek R1–R4 preconditions** — R1, R2, R3 (two non-adjacent mediators c→b, d→b with a—c, a—d) and R4 (a—c→d→b, a adj d, c not adj b) match Meek 1995. R4's premise string says "discriminating path" (cosmetic).
- **Discriminating-path definition** — `is_discriminating_path` requires every intermediate to be a collider on the path AND a definite parent (tail–arrow) of b, a∉adj(b), circle at c on c–b, and R4 consults Sep(a,b). Correct. (Budget behaviour is discovery-21.)
- **MCI lag shift off-by-one** — `mci_conditioning` shifts source parents by exactly `source_lag` and frames are built to `2·max_lag` (engine.rs:441, 1145); correct for every call with `target_lag=0`. (The lagged-target caller is discovery-15.)
- **Thread-count determinism (`engine.rs:576,671`)** — per-target work is independent, results are written to index-addressed slots / concatenated in range order, PC1 ties are broken by (var,lag), BlockShuffle is forced onto the 1-query path to keep RNG salts identical, MCI batches are per target so query indices do not depend on chunking. Discovered graph is independent of thread count. MCMC chains use `ctx.rng.stream(base+chain)`, independent of chunking.
- **Structure-MCMC Hastings ratio** — picking an ordered pair uniformly from a list containing both (i,j) and (j,i), with 50/50 delete/reverse, gives q(add)=q(delete)=q(reverse)=1/m in both directions; ratio 1 is correct. Acyclicity is checked on every add/reverse.
- **Order-MCMC** — it is really a joint (order, DAG) sampler; the target divides by the number of linear extensions (`log_topological_order_count`, subset DP), which removes the classic order-MCMC bias; adjacent-swap, arbitrary-swap (with skeleton re-orientation) and order-consistent edge toggle are each symmetric. Correct. (Name is a misnomer.)
- **Exact enumeration cap** — hard refusal above 6 nodes with a pointer to MCMC; DAG counts 1,1,3,25,543,29281,3781503 are right; memory pre-check present.
- **Gaussian BIC sign/penalty** — `−n/2(1+ln2π+lnσ̂²) − k/2·ln n`, higher is better, k=|Pa|+1; GES deltas are Chickering Cor. 16/18; Insert validity (NA∪T clique, semi-directed Y⇝X blocked by NA∪T) and Delete validity (NA∖H clique) are right; a turning phase exists. (The defect is in the PDAG→DAG step, discovery-4.)
- **CI NaN handling in PC/FCI/RFCI/PCMCI** — every edge is first tested with Z=∅, where a NaN column yields a non-finite statistic and the run is refused; the "r=0 for NaN with non-empty Z" parcorr behaviour is therefore not reachable first. All engine CI sites check `is_finite` and propagate. The exceptions are discovery-5 (majority rule) and discovery-8 (LiNGAM).
- **RFCI Lemma 3.1 / 3.2** — triple re-queueing after edge removal, sepset minimisation, and the "all subsets of Sep(a,b)∖{u,v} for each consecutive pair" check follow Colombo–Maathuis.
- **Possible-D-SEP** — BFS over (node, predecessor) states with collider-or-triangle triples is right; FCI tests subsets of pds(x)∪pds(y) (superset of the standard family — sound). FCI/RFCI refuse FDR because the adaptive family is incomplete — good.
- **`rpcmci.rs:462-496` / `lpcmci_phases.rs:686` as perf problems** — linear scans over ≤ tens of variables, outside CI-dominated cost.
- **Soundness/completeness over-claims in docs** — none found; FCI/RFCI/NOTEARS/LiNGAM headers describe deviations (dCor instead of T_kernel, hard threshold disclaimer, rules omitted). The gap is in the *result object* (discovery-7), not the prose.

### docs-claims — reviewed and sound

- README / index / system-model / causal-contract / result-is-a-claim / refusal-and-partial-knowledge / guarantees: scope language is careful ("does not guarantee that a causal model describes reality", "bounds are not confidence intervals", capability ≠ license ≠ validity). Only defect is what they delegate to (`result.calibration`, docs-claims-1).
- docs/short-series-thresholds.md gate arithmetic: ±3 MCSE bands [0.855,0.945] at 400/0.90 and [0.917,0.983] at 0.95 are correct (MCSE 0.015 and 0.0109); precision floor 0.887 at 2000/0.90 = 0.90−2·0.0067 correct; 0.940 at 0.95 correct.
- docs/capabilities.md logit-interval formula for atom probabilities (`expit(logit p̂ ± z·se/(p̂(1−p̂)))`) is the correct delta-method form.
