### estimate-bayes-transport-response — refuted leads

- `bayesian.rs:1153` `seed.wrapping_add(n_draws)`: `CausalRng` is SplitMix64 with `state = seed ^ G`; two seeds differing by a small integer start at offsets `Δ·G⁻¹ mod 2⁶⁴` apart in the counter sequence (astronomically far), and the block seed is deterministic given `(seed, min_draws, batch)`. No overlap with the initial backend stream (`n_draws ≥ 2`). Fine.
- `bayesian.rs:734` `(active−control).abs() < f64::EPSILON`: only rejects contrasts below 2.2e-16; hydrate separately floors at 1e-12. Inconsistent constants but no realistic failure.
- `bayesian.rs:97-98` casts: `level` is validated in (0,1) by every caller (`response::validate`, hard-coded 0.95), so `p·(n−1) ∈ [0, n−1]`; no OOB.
- `bayesian.rs:1429` weights: `−ln(U)` iid Exp(1), normalised by their sum → Dirichlet(1,…,1); `U` floored at `MIN_POSITIVE`. Correct Rubin Bayesian bootstrap. Same for `response::bootstrap_weights` (`:2007`); `total` cannot be 0 for n ≥ 20.
- `bayesian.rs:1496-1501` ANOVA `n0 = (N − Σn_i²/N)/(g−1)`: the standard unbalanced one-way MOM coefficient; correct. (Residual-based MSB ignores regressor df — acceptable for a plug-in.)
- `bayesian_mediation.rs`: mediator/outcome fits use different seeds (`+0xBA71` at the caller), so `a` and `b` draws are independent; `n = min(len)` truncation is consistent across direct/indirect/total; `±0.0` contrast guard is right.
- `transport.rs` weights: inverse odds `(1−s)/s` for the nonparticipant target, Horvitz (not Hájek) normalised by `n_target`, matching Dahabreh IOW1 / augmented estimator; selection and treatment overlap are reported separately. SE algebra verified (Σ IF = 0 exactly).
- `statistical_transport.rs:515` `expect("known outcome")`: map is built from the same `outcomes` slice; unreachable.
- `statistical_transport.rs` resampling: one index vector per bound dataset key per replicate (`row_indexes` keyed by `bound_sample_key`, duplicates overwrite), independent streams per dataset `(dataset<<32)|replicate`, all grid points evaluated on the same assembled replicate, shared `replicate_ids`; a replicate counts only if every grid point succeeds. Sound. (`percentile_interval` returning `(NaN,NaN)` on a non-finite replicate is unreachable here because evaluation errors are counted as failures first.)
- `learned_trial.rs`: bootstrap duplicates keep their original fold (`draw_folds`), so no cross-fold leakage of duplicated rows; any failed replicate withholds the interval.
- `response/mod.rs:1361`: ADE variance uses `n−1`. `:1197` uses `n(n−1)` on centred ψ. Fine.
- `response/mod.rs:1428,1464` `unwrap_or_default()`: empty `outcomes`/`treatments` are rejected by `ResponseQuery::validate` (`query/response.rs:767`), so `multivariate_support` never divides by zero.
- Simultaneous band (`band.rs`): Rademacher multiplier max-|t| over the fixed grid using the same influence rows and SEs as the pointwise band (so joint covariance is respected), `⌈level·(B+1)⌉` rank, floored at the pointwise z. Correct for the claimed "fixed-grid, conditional on pseudo-outcome" scope; ≥100 replicates and explicit bandwidth enforced.

### estimate-bayes-transport-response — reviewed and sound

- Kennedy DR pseudo-outcome (`:1491-1638`): ξ = m̄(A) + (Y−μ̂)·π̄(A)/π̂(A|X) with fold-local marginals; additive-μ hoist `μ̂(a,X_j) = fitted_j − f_T(A_j) + f_T(a)` is exact; density floor counted and surfaced; covariate-marginalisation IF term `(c_i − c̄)/n` added.
- Gaussian-score Riesz ADE (`:1640-1682`): α = (A − m̂(X))/σ², score `∂_a μ̂ + α(Y−μ̂)`; σ uses `n − edf`.
- Penalised g-computation sandwich (`:2534-2645`): dropped-last-basis reparametrisation with truncated D2'D2 penalty is equivalent under the penalty's constant-shift invariance; ψ = (m_i − m̄) + n·e_i·x_i'(X'X+λP)⁻¹g, centred; rank check refuses unidentifiable designs.
- `inflate_draws_by_edf` HC1 argument and the outcome index `j·S/dim`.
- `transform_point_derivative` chain rules for log-treatment / log-outcome / log-log second derivatives (checked symbolically).
- `gcomp_mean_contrast` (identity shortcut and nonlinear-link row average) and `add_modifier_mean_uncertainty` (centred-interaction design, column 2 = modifier, BB draw of μ_w − w̄).
- `random_intercept_gls_whiten` transform `λ = 1 − 1/√(1 + n_i τ²/σ²)` (Gaussian case).
- AIPW transport score (`transport.rs:268-321`) and `transport_augmented_response_grid` algebra as "evaluates the supplied equation" (no DR claim made).

### estimate-static — refuted leads

- **`se.rs` demeaning vs `antecedent-stats/covariance.rs` (not demeaned, extra `(n−1)/(n−p)`)** — consistent. Regression scores `x_i e_i` are orthogonal to the design (incl. intercept) so need no demeaning; an IF with estimated mean must be centred, which `cluster_meat_scalar(.., mean)` does. With p=1 the Stata factor `(n−1)/(n−p)` is 1, so `G/(G−1)/n²·Σ s_g²` is the same convention. Formulas verified: HC1 `Σ(ψ−ψ̄)²/(n(n−1))`; HC0 × `(n−1)/n`; cluster `G/(G−1)·Σ_g s_g²/n²`; NW `[γ₀+2Σ(1−k/(L+1))γ_k]/n` with γ_k/n normalisation; panel HAC `G/(G−1)·M/n²`.
- **`multiway_influence_se` negative inclusion–exclusion** — handled: `combine_inclusion_exclusion` clamps only `|neg| ≤ 64ε·Σ|term|`, otherwise returns `NonPositiveVariance` (refusal, not a silent 0).
- **`se.rs:415` `grouped.last().unwrap()`** — unreachable panic: guarded by `n ≥ 2` so the map is non-empty.
- **`dml.rs:334-338` `(φ−ate)·n/kept`** — correct. With zeros for dropped rows, `iid_se` (÷n) gives `Σ_kept(φ−θ)²·n/((n−1)k²)` = `s²_kept/k` up to `(k−1)n/(k(n−1))`. Consumers (`joint_influence_covariance`, `batch.rs::embed_centered_influence`) all divide by n. (DRY note: `embed_centered_influence` in `crates/antecedent/src/analysis/batch.rs:1061` repeats the same embedding.)
- **PLR SE** — `θ=Σ D̃Ỹ/Σ D̃²`, `ψ_i=(D̃Ỹ−θD̃²)/(Σ D̃²/n)`, `se = sd(ψ)/√n` = `√(E[ψ²]/J²/n)`; trimmed rows contribute 0 with J over the n-universe. Correct. Constant-effect caveat is recorded as an assumption. (Label nit only: tagged `Homoskedastic` though it is a robust IF SE.)
- **`hajek_influence_se` "ignores estimated propensity / conservative"** — refuted: stacked-M correction `B′I⁻¹x(T−e)` with target-specific `∂log w/∂η` (−(1−e), e, 1, −1, 0 beyond clip) verified by hand; a case-weight perturbation oracle test exists. Only oddity: ad-hoc `n/(n−p)` inflation.
- **AIPW score / ATT / ATC forms** (`aipw.rs:706-740`, `center_population_psi`) — match the standard DR scores and the Hahn EIF `(N_i − τT_i)/π`.
- **Hájek vs HT** — IPW is Hájek (`hajek_difference`), AIPW scores are plain means of DR scores; weighted targets use self-normalised weights in `joint_if::weighted_mean`. Consistent.
- **Bootstrap parallel splitter (`util.rs:302-368`)** — result independent of thread count: all index plans are pre-generated in one batch from `(ctx, stream_base)`, replicate r always reads slice r, results collected in replicate order. (Memory: `n·B` u32 up front — 4 GB at n=10⁶, B=1000; guarded by `try_reserve_exact`.)
- **`joint_if.rs:207-211` weighted covariance** — `n/(n−1)·Σ(w̃_i(φ_i−θ))²` equals `Σψ²/n²` for `ψ_i = n w̃_i(φ_i−θ)`; correct. `:342` NaN-tolerant sort is unreachable-NaN (finite Cholesky × finite normals). `:393` `w.round() as usize` exact for integer block weights. `cholesky_corr` handles PSD-singular correlation (zero pivot ⇒ zero column, error on inconsistent off-diagonals).
- **`util.rs:22 solve_spd`** — thin wrapper over stats Cholesky; fine.
- **`cell_aipw.rs:487` "Dirichlet weights" / `:666-669` "EPSILON dedup"** — stale line refs; `:487` is a `ln(max(p, MIN_POSITIVE))` log-likelihood guard (fine), `:666` is a threshold-count helper. No Dirichlet sampler in this file.
- **`adjustment.rs:1013` "bootstrap SE × z"** — stale; that location is `bootstrap_se`, which only returns the replicate SD. Interval construction is outside this unit.
- **`propensity/stratification.rs:272` NaN-tolerant sort** — scores come from a GLM fit that passed `require_ok`; NaN not reachable in practice. Ties are split by stable index order (deterministic).
- **`propensity/matching.rs:446` `expect("ensured")`, `:625` `unwrap_or(0.0)`** — unreachable (`ensure_matching_index` just ran; `effect_rows` index the same restricted universe as `tw_used`).

### estimate-static — reviewed and sound

- `se.rs`: all five scalar-IF variance estimators, `influence_se_kind` row-map gathering, calendar-time NW with contemporaneous aggregation (test at `:553` checks a hand value).
- `propensity/weighting.rs`: IPW weights for ATE/ATT/ATC, Hájek ratio, stacked-M SE, `solve_symmetric_posdef` (scale-relative pivot tolerance).
- `dml.rs`: AIPW and PLR scores, Jacobian normalisation, trimmed-universe embedding.
- `joint_if.rs`: weighted mean/covariance scaling invariance, Kish n_eff, frozen-weight mixture, PAVA, max-t via Cholesky of the correlation matrix.
- `util.rs`: bootstrap failure accounting (`>50%` failures ⇒ no SE), cancellation semantics, `delta_method_se`, `ols_sigma2`.
- `glm_adjustment.rs`: g-computation gradient, probit Fisher weight `φ²/(μ(1−μ))`, score-based sandwich for all five families (the NB defect is only in the model-based path). Conditional-on-X SE is documented as such; the omitted `Var(τ(X))/n` term is ≤~1% of the variance for typical logistic fits.
- `conditional.rs` linear path: delta-method SE including the `β_TW²·Var(w̄)` term, per-arm plug-in IFs.
- `cell_aipw.rs`: reference-cell multinomial logit gradient/Hessian, softmax overflow guard, missing-training-cell refusal.
- `crossfit_aipw.rs`: train/valid separation, per-fold arm check, raw propensities retained for the overlap report.
- `overlap.rs`: report construction, clip-sensitivity grid uses the production weight formula.
- `retarget.rs`: depends-on/descendant refusal logic, weighted summary, monotone rearrangement flagged via diagnostic.

### estimate-structural — refuted leads

- `iv.rs:760 analytic_se_2sls` "residuals from fitted T": refuted. `fit_2sls` (`antecedent-stats/src/twosls.rs:157-169`) builds `structural_residuals`/`structural_rss` from the ACTUAL endogenous regressor; bread uses X̂. Robust kinds pass X̂ + structural residuals to the sandwich, which is the correct 2SLS HC form.
- `iv.rs:460 wald_influence_scores`: correct IF of a ratio of arm-mean contrasts, `((ψ_dy − r·ψ_dt)/Δt)` with `n/n_z` scaling; SE = sd(ψ)/√n.
- `iv.rs:329,702` bootstrap: failed replicates are counted (`BootstrapSeResult.replicates_failed`), SE withheld when more than half fail; not silently dropped.
- First-stage F: partial F for the excluded block with restricted fit on exogenous regressors, df1 = #instruments, df2 = n − k₁ — correct.
- `frontdoor.rs` multi-mediator path sum (incl. chained mediators M1→M2): correct for linear SEMs; stacked M-estimator sandwich with block-diagonal bread and empirical cross-blocks is correct; `:5` "guarantees" describes the criterion, not the estimator.
- `rd.rs:347` `unwrap_or(NaN)`: unreachable (`residual_sandwich_coef_se` returns `None` only for Homoskedastic, excluded by the match arm). RD docs honestly state no bandwidth selector, uniform kernel, no bias correction, and push that as an Estimation-scope assumption.
- `static_mediation.rs:347` `seed.wrapping_add(i+1)`: `CausalRng` is SplitMix64 with state `seed ^ γ`; adjacent seeds give sequences offset by γ⁻¹ mod 2⁶⁴ steps — no practical overlap. Sequential-ignorability/no-interaction reliance is recorded (`mediation.additive_linear`, `mediation.no_interaction`).
- `quantile.rs:78` clamp: weight is already in (0,1] by construction (`f_lo < τ ≤ f_hi`); harmless. `:241` nearest-rank picks grid thresholds, not a reported statistic. IF `−φ_F/f` with linear interpolation verified by differentiating `q = t_lo + w·width`.
- `functional_distribution.rs:177-195`: p∈{0,1}, se=None, se=0 all map to explicit `Unavailable` reasons. `:441` NaN is followed by `!tracked.is_finite() → Ok(None)`. `:1317` Exp(1) weights with `max(MIN_POSITIVE)` are a correct Rubin Dirichlet(1,…,1) after the CPT's own normalisation. `:1466` `total > 0` is checked at `:1450`. Missing atoms in a replicate are set to 0 when their conditioning stratum is present (`align_replicate_atoms`), so rare-atom SEs are not truncated.
- `identified_set.rs`: Imbens–Manski critical value `Φ(c+Δ/σ_max) − Φ(−c) = level` is right; bisection bracket [z_level, z_{(1+level)/2}] is valid (checked both ends); coverage argument in the module doc checks out (convexity in d_l, bound via σ_max); moment-selection threshold avoids the Stoye superefficiency problem. `:363` non-finite filter is dead code (see finding 9).
- `envelope.rs:115,178,326`: `total>0` checked at `:93`; index cast is bounded (`u<1`, plus `min`); `(n−1).max(1)` fine. Mixture moments are the exact law-of-total-variance moments; identification label follows the `GraphIdentFlag::Identified` = "nonparametrically identified" contract.
- `interference.rs:65` `seed+1`: independent enough (see SplitMix note). Conservative-variance claim verified: Young bound gives Var(HT mean) ≤ (1/n)ΣVar(X_i), estimated unbiasedly by Σ 1{E_i}(1−π_i)y_i²/π_i² / n; contrast bound 2(V_a+V_b) is valid. (It bounds the HT estimator; the Hájek value published beside it has no separate variance — worth a doc line in `antecedent-stats`.)
- `empirical_table.rs:428` `expect("validated regime")`: `catalog_axes_bounded` at `:425` already returned an error for an unknown regime. `:94` NaN only for `Value::Label`, which is rejected later by the finite-value check at `:436`.
- `calibration_coverage.rs` "compiled into the library": refuted — `lib.rs:66` gates it with `#[cfg(test)]`. `:165` divisor is report-only. Scoring treats a non-finite SE as a miss (good).

### estimate-structural — reviewed and sound

- 2SLS point estimate, structural residuals, homoskedastic and sandwich SE wiring (`iv.rs` + `twosls.rs`).
- Wald ratio, first-stage two-sample F, ratio influence function.
- Front-door stacked sandwich: bread, HC0 meat, cluster meat, delta-method gradient.
- Sharp RD local-linear design `[1,T,R−c,T(R−c)]`, jump coefficient, HC wiring, 0/1 level restriction.
- Static mediation path propagation (total vs mediator-blocked direct) on a DAG; bootstrap soft-failure accounting.
- Piecewise-linear CDF inversion and its influence function; isotonic-projection refusal at the bracket.
- Logit delta-method probability interval; Bayesian-bootstrap CPT provider (one row law per draw).
- Imbens–Manski interval (frequentist shared-replicate and posterior-envelope variants).
- BMA envelope moments and mixture sampler; Iman–Conover rank coupling (marginals preserved, mean equals BMA mean).
- Design-based HT/Hájek exposure contrast glue and conservative variance bound.
- Selected-outcome cross-fitted AIPW pseudo-values: folds exclude the evaluated row, fold failure refuses instead of falling back in-sample, uncertainty withheld with an explicit warning.
- Trial-to-target AIPW resampling design (nested vs independent samples, folds follow source rows, interval withheld on any replicate failure).

### estimate-temporal — refuted leads

- `temporal_block.rs:441` circular cubic has "no citation": it has in-file provenance (in-house simulation) and I reproduced it. CBB-studentised |t| 95% quantiles, iid N(0,1), n = 300, 30 000 reps (`et_fixedb.py`): b = 0.1/0.2/0.3/0.4 → 2.218/2.582/2.971/3.386 vs polynomial 2.239/2.579/2.968/3.394 (KV non-circular 2.261/2.566/2.874/3.180). Correct and applied to the circular (bootstrap) variance only.
- `temporal_block.rs:522` is the Kiefer–Vogelsang (2005) Bartlett two-sided 95% polynomial and is applied only to the non-circular Newey–West bound in `serial_dependence.rs:593` with `b = (L+1)/n`, matching the Bartlett weight `1 − j/(L+1)` at :1550. Other levels are not reachable: all temporal bands hard-code 0.95 and scalar SE intervals use `REPORTED_SE_INTERVAL_LEVEL = 0.95`.
- Block bootstrap implementation (`antecedent-data/src/resample.rs:227-253`): circular wrap `(start+k) % n`, n possible starts, last block truncated so resample length is exactly n. Correct CBB. Lag-aligned rows are contiguous because `plan.prepare` rejects missing/masked series.
- Politis–White (`temporal_block.rs:256-315`): K_n = max(5, ⌈√log10 n⌉), m_max = ⌈√n⌉ + K_n, threshold 2√(log10 n / n), M = 2m̂, flat-top window, Ĝ = 2Σ k λ γ_k, D_CB = (4/3)ĝ(0)², cap ⌈min(3√n, n/3)⌉ — matches Patton–Politis–White 2009. Float→usize casts are on finite, clamped, non-negative values.
- HAC bandwidth `⌊4(n/100)^{2/9}⌋` (serial_dependence.rs:1539); lag 0 counted once, lags 1..L doubled with Bartlett weights (:1544-1554). No double count.
- κ̂: floored at 1 (`clamp(1.0, cap)` :532) and capped at n/(p+2) with `capped` flag; AR order is BIC-selected 0..=min(4, n−p−2), not fixed at 4; n < max(8, p+2) returns κ = 1 with `inestimable = true` and honest wording (but see finding 2 for where that wording is lost). "Calibrated" wording is accompanied by the measured 0.87–0.92 range and the long-memory caveat.
- `temporal_response.rs:1092` / `temporal_sequential.rs:994` / `temporal_observed_bayes.rs:674` additive seeds: `CausalRng` is SplitMix64 (counter + strong output mix); additive seed offsets give counter offsets whose sequences overlap only after ~2^63 draws. Acceptable.
- `temporal_response.rs:1558` `normal_ppf(0.975)` matches the hard-coded 0.95 band level; `:2054` uses n−1 (correct sample SD); `:352` cast is of `ceil(sqrt(n))`.
- `temporal_response_dispersion.rs:269-285` BIC `n ln σ̂² + q ln n` is the standard Yule–Walker BIC; `variance <= 0 || NaN` breaks the order search rather than substituting a value.
- `temporal_mediation/shared.rs:162-262` `weight/total`: all three uses are behind the single guard at :162 (`total.is_nan() || total <= 0.0` → `None`).
- `temporal_observed_bayes.rs:934` `quantile`: input length is `4 × max(1024, …)` ≥ 4096, never empty; p ∈ {0.025, 0.975}.
- `temporal_sequential.rs` expects (:616, :618, :899, :917, :921, :1018, :1041): indices come from the same indexer/unfolding that produced them; lag `child − parent ≥ 0` in an acyclic time-ordered unfolding. Not user-reachable. `CompiledDesign::linear_adjustment` preserves covariate order, so `coefficients[i][p+1]` indexing in `propagate` is right.
- `temporal_observed_bayes.rs` sampler: full conditional of a latent y_t combines its own mechanism prior with every child equation that has y at that lag (precision += b²/σ², linear += b(x_child − mean_without)/σ²) — correct; truncated-normal sampler is Robert (1995) with α* = (a + √(a²+4))/2 and correct acceptance; Upper bound handled by reflection.
- Horizon conventions: effect/response `outcome_offset = horizon − 1` is used consistently in `temporal_adjustment`, `temporal_observed_bayes` (:418-421) and the Sequence caller; mediation's `treatment_lag = horizon` is its own documented convention and the Study runs one query per horizon.

### estimate-temporal — reviewed and sound

- `serial_dependence.rs`: PACF→AR Levinson step-up, Yule–Walker solve for ρ, stationary-block Cholesky whitener, `correlate = L⁻¹L'⁻¹/γ₀`, `RemlKernel` lagged cross-product bookkeeping (S_jk boundary subtraction ranges), profile REML likelihood `−½log|Γ| − ½log|X̃'X̃| − ½(n−p)log(RSS/(n−p))`, BIC with ln(n−p), Nelder–Mead + fixed-Hessian Newton polish, delta-method τ̂ from the central-difference stencil, early-exit ordering over combinations, content-verified fit cache.
- `temporal_block.rs`: `effective_rows`, `block_effective_rows`, `kernel_bias_scale` (expected Bartlett under AR(1)), `aligned_block_bootstrap` time mapping, joint-drop of non-finite replicates.
- `temporal_response.rs`: `max_deviation_band_by` (sup-t, non-varying-cell guard, floor at z), `bootstrap_surface` flat layout and failure accounting, `block_dispersion_inflation`.
- `temporal_mediation.rs`: Frisch–Waugh contrast scores and delta-method `b·ψ_a + a·ψ_b`; Sobel variance; shared-replicate SEs.
- `temporal_sequential.rs`: needed-set traversal with hard-intervention edge cutting, anchor/lag computation, stationary-mechanism single fit for the Bayesian path, contrast gradient by exact central differences of a multilinear map.

### expr — refuted leads

- **`exact.rs:392` float→Int64 cast**: guarded by `is_finite && fract()==0 && |x| ≤ 2^53`; no truncation/saturation, −0.0→0, no collision between distinct Int64 and Float64 levels. Sound.
- **Stride / index computation**: strides built in reverse (last axis fastest, matching the doc); `mass()` full-index fast path requires all axes distinct; partial path `(i/stride)%len==level` correct; `P(X|X)` degenerates to 1 correctly. Domain order differences between laws are harmless (per-law `level_index`).
- **`(a/b)/c → a/(b·c)`**: same domain of definition (b≠0 ∧ c≠0 ⇔ b·c≠0) except float underflow of `b·c` (`1e-200·1e-200=0` → spurious refusal, never a wrong value; exact engine reports "product underflow"). `a/(b/c)` is deliberately not rewritten (correct reasoning in the comment). An inner `Extendable` was already fatal under the outer Ratio, so the rewrite does not lose extensions. Product re-sorting changes results only at ulp level / underflow-order level.
- **Variable capture in merge / substitution**: `merge_sum_out` refuses overlapping binders (test covers multiplicity); `substitute` checks injectivity on free variables, capture by enclosing binders, and conflicting intervention values. Sound.
- **Hash-consing across populations/regimes**: `ExprNode::Distribution`/`Kernel` include `population` and `regime` in Eq/Hash; the simplifier has no rule that merges leaves. Not mergeable in the arena (the merge happens in the provider — expr-5).
- **`u32::try_from(..).expect`**: needs > 2³² interned items (≫100 GB); not practically reachable.
- **Budget exhaustion as a value**: every exhaustion/cancellation path returns `Err`; `evaluate` builds the probability vector locally and returns nothing on failure. SumOut loops are charged through `slot()`; provider scans are charged by `factor_work`.
- **NaN in the exact path**: law axes, law interventions and the request are all validated finite, so a NaN datum cannot select an intervention world there (the alias is real only in `CompiledEvaluator`, expr-2).
- **sampling_zero vs zero_conditioning_mass at leaf level**: classification is right for leaves (counts checked before mass; non-supplied origins never yield the extendable kind); errors are never inserted in the shared factor cache.
- **LawTolerance**: `unit_mass` and the target normalisation check are consistent (`abs+rel` around 1); no renormalisation is performed anywhere.

### expr — reviewed and sound

- Neumaier sum (`exact::sum`) and the inline Kahan loop in `ExactSession`.
- `ExactDiscreteLaw::try_new` validation order and `ExactTransportData::try_new` world index / domain compatibility.
- Zero-mass extension algebra in `Product` (non-negativity + bounded extension ⇒ extension-independent zero), including underflow/overflow guards.
- `ExactSession` cache keying by projection onto per-slot free variables (concretely bound interventions are overwritten in scope, so excluding them from the key is correct).
- `with_scoped_bindings` restore-on-error semantics; `compute_free_vars` ≡ `simplify::free_vars` (same Distribution rule in both).
- Gauss–Hermite n=5 nodes/weights and the Lebesgue re-weighting `w·σ√2·e^{t²}`.
- `Preflight::leaf_cost` world enumeration (mixed-radix decode) and `support()` Cartesian decode.

### facade-analysis — refuted leads

- **`validate_uncertainty` duplicated in `antecedent-io/src/causal_artifact.rs:966`** — same name, unrelated function (that one validates `ResponseUncertaintyWire` band shapes). Not a duplicate, nothing to drift.
- **Replicate accounting not as documented** — it is as documented: estimator `ok < 2 || failed/attempted > 0.5` (`statistical_transport.rs:253-255`), facade contrast and consume use `failed/requested` and consume enforces `ok + failed == requested` (`statistical.rs:1387`), so the two denominators coincide. (The rule itself is too weak — finding 4.)
- **Dependence contract** — unknown / clustered / weighted dependence keeps Identification = identified and withholds the interval with `transport.unsupported_dependence`; tests `:1717`, `:1803` exercise it. Sound apart from finding 10.
- **`calibration_status: not_bound_to_this_execution` logic** — there is no logic in scope: `calibration_binding` is always `None` from the estimator and consume rejects `Some` (`statistical.rs:1385`). The string is a constant in `python/src/transport_*_api.rs`.
- **`refresh`/`replace_snapshot` can leave a stale claim exportable** — no. Sampled handle: result stamps carry the data-snapshot digest (precomputed `content_digest` covers columns, analysis mask and weights); `contract_for_result` refuses on snapshot or program mismatch; a mixed-execution refute is left unstamped. Transport handles: `export` compares `result.identities` (includes snapshot + inference binding) with the handle's. Refresh builds a candidate, executes it, and only then swaps `*self` in all five modalities; failure leaves the previous state (tested at `prepared.rs:3917`).
- **`inspect()` clones datasets / refits** — no. `Study::inspect` -> `compile_contract(self, None)` reads no cache, runs no identification, and the snapshot layer uses the storage's precomputed digest; `TabularData` clones are `Arc<[OwnedColumn]>` bumps. Statistical/exact `inspect` are metadata only.
- **Identity from Debug/Display strings** — none. `contract_identity.rs` maps floats via `to_bits`, enums via explicit `&'static str` matches, vectors via payload digests. The `format!` strings in `contract.rs` (`functional_label`, `overlap_label`, `posterior_label`) are calibration match keys, deliberately coarse, not identities.
- **`latency.rs` timing-dependent results** — none. `wall_ms` is advisory and unread; `Instant` is used only for stage timing diagnostics (`stage.rs`, `prepared.rs:2137,2170`). Replicate/draw counts are deterministic functions of the tier.
- **`batch.rs:586,652,1205` clones** — Arc-backed, ignore as the lead said.
- **`exact.rs` budgets/cancellation** — preflight memory check, per-point operation limits, `plan.evaluate` errors propagate; consume re-evaluates and compares support, atoms, probabilities and reasoning. No partial claim escapes.

### facade-analysis — reviewed and sound

- `transport_grid.rs::consume` (`:859-1104`): recomputes — proof check against rebuilt diagram, catalog binding, grid validation, every `Unavailable` point is re-executed and must fail with the *same* located failure, every executable point is independently consumed (exact: full re-evaluation; statistical: point recomputed from embedded fitted joints, intervals recomputed from embedded replicates) and its proof/catalog/laws/request/grid/samples/options/seed must equal the family's; v2 point-evidence digests and the manifest identity are re-derived. Trusted: the bootstrap replicates themselves (documented "does not re-bootstrap"). Partial grids keep failed coordinates in order with counts in the Support slot; `contrast` and `scalar_projection` refuse unavailable points; the scalar carries a `HandoffReceipt::lossy_scalar`. A grid with failed points cannot look complete.
- `statistical.rs::validate_uncertainty` (`:1341-1472`): row fields bound to options/seed/sample sizes, strictly increasing replicate ids < requested, per-row simplex check (1e-9), intervals recomputed bit-exactly, operation budget checked with `checked_mul`.
- `StatisticalStudyResult::contrast` (`:75-155`): pairs replicates by id so shared-sample covariance is preserved; refuses across different identification / snapshot / inference binding.
- Prepared lifecycle identity chain: `executed_contract` -> `contract_and_payloads_for_result` -> row-weights rebinding -> snapshot-then-program conflict ordering.
- `embed_centered_influence` + `joint_influence_covariance` convention (modulo finding 15); `max_t_critical` (fixed seed 1, 4096 draws, deterministic).
- `project_conditional_cdf` (per-arm isotonic + clamp, change flag).
- Builder conflict checks for bootstrap/overlap set on both builder and configured estimator; `compute_budget` correctly marks fields explicit.

### facade-execute — refuted leads

- **`bayesian_path.rs:2364,2830` shared seed for every `i ≥ 1`**: `preparations` is `[PreparedBayesianProblem; 2]` (`antecedent-estimate/src/bayesian_mediation.rs:48`) — mediator mechanism and outcome mechanism. There is never an `i = 2`, so the two fits get `seed` and `seed+0xBA71`; no streams are shared. (Fragile if the array ever grows; `wrapping_add(0xBA71 * i)` would be future-proof.)
- **`compile.rs` / `dispatch.rs` 85 `unreachable!()` user-reachable?** No. In every arm the `AnalysisRoute` is computed by `classify_analysis_route(&self.data, &self.query)` in the *same expression* as the match, and `classify_route` (`execute_helpers.rs:76-112`) maps each route from exactly the `(DataModality, CausalQuery variant)` the arm destructures; `data_modality` is a total 1:1 map. The remaining ones (`bayesian_path.rs:2247`, `temporal_class_mediation_posterior.rs:274`, `temporal_path.rs:784,1474`) are in private fns behind an `InferenceMode::Bayesian` check on the same `&self`; `transport_interference_path.rs:234` follows an explicit refusal. Refactor sketch: make `classify_route` return `enum Routed<'a> { StaticAte{data:&'a TabularData,q:&'a AverageEffectQuery}, TemporalEffect{data:&'a TimeSeriesData,q:&'a TemporalEffectQuery}, Panel…{panel:&'a PanelData,..}, … }` with `fn kind(&self)->AnalysisRoute` for the licensing tables that key on the plain enum. Blast radius: `compile.rs` (59 sites, 3 fns), `dispatch.rs` (26 sites, 2 fns), `execute_helpers.rs` (`classify_*`, `is_gcm_route`), and the builder's route-licensing checks that call `classify_route(modality, query)` without data (keep that signature as `kind`-only). Purely mechanical; no behaviour change.
- **`panel_path.rs` random-intercept whitening under Poisson/Bernoulli**: refused upstream. `builder.rs:730-775` `refuse_unsupported_likelihood` licenses a non-Gaussian likelihood only for `DataInput::Tabular` + `GraphClass::Dag` + fixed structure + `AverageEffect` mean; panels, temporal, class envelopes and graph posteriors all return `likelihood_not_supported`. `bprep.unit_ids` is only set on panel paths. (Holds as long as `Study` values are only built through the builder.)
- **`response_path.rs:1341-1343` `/ total_mass.max(EPSILON)`**: reached only after `total_w > 0` (or the sole-unevaluable-atom branch), and `total_mass = identified + unidentified ≥ total_w`; the three masses sum to one. The guard is dead but harmless.
- **`temporal_path.rs:1693` hard-coded z**: paired with a hard-coded `level: 0.95` in the same function, and only used when the posterior is absent; in the Frequentist branch `estimate_sequence_mechanisms` is called with 0 replicates and returns `se_analytic = NaN` (`temporal_sequential.rs:1108-1110`), so the z-band is never finite and the band comes from the tuple bootstrap (which uses `reported_se_interval_z()`). Duplicate constant only.
- **`temporal_path.rs:4641` private `sample_sd`**: guarded by `bootstrap_has_enough_successes` (n ≥ 2), n−1 denominator is correct. DRY duplicate of the estimator crate's SD, no defect. **`:4474` linear `contains`**: over an adjustment set (tens of entries), once per run.
- **`mod.rs:858`** is a test, not the mixture implementation. The implementation: frequentist multi-atom SE is deliberately NaN unless a joint IF / shared bootstrap exists (`mix_weighted_analytic_se`, `execute_helpers.rs:864-879`); Bayesian mixing pools draws via `aggregate_effect_envelope` (between-graph variance included by construction); unidentified mass is carried on the posterior, forces `GraphDependent`, and disagreeing estimands withhold the scalar (`resolve_structural_aggregation`). Not silently renormalised — except for the conflation in finding 5.
- **Refuter failures hidden?** `RefutationReport::mixture_weighted` is fail-closed (`antecedent-validate/src/common.rs:116-157`); `run_refuters` errors propagate with `?`. The gap is partial-NA coverage (finding 6), not hidden failures.
- **Multi-horizon DBN mediation status upgrade** (`bayesian_path.rs:2197-2203`): parent status is `most_conservative_identification_status` over slices; unidentified horizons get NaN slices with `NotIdentified`. Sound. (Minor: `cancelled: false` is hard-coded there.)

### facade-execute — reviewed and sound

- `mix_weighted_analytic_se`, `mix_static_envelope_se`, `mixed_static_influence` (frozen-weight joint-IF SE; NaN on any non-finite / multi-atom without joint scores).
- `panel_cluster_se_fit` + `few_cluster_t_ratio` scaling (NaN below 2 clusters; G−1 df; disclosed in the unit-bootstrap diagnostic).
- `shared_circular_block_mixture_se` window alignment (`offset = start − first_time`, replicate dropped rather than renormalised when any atom fails).
- `summarize_observation_bootstrap` band arithmetic (centre = full-sample point, SD with n−1) apart from the replicate floor in finding 4.
- `sequential_validation.rs` stand-in logic: NA under informative prior, NA when |LS − posterior mean| > 0.25 SD, and the report text states it does not test the credible interval.
- `ensure_supported_combination` / `classify_route` exhaustiveness for the unreachable analysis.
- Conditional-effect grid mixing (`execute_helpers.rs:760-830`): scalar cleared for multi-threshold grids, SE cleared when any threshold unsupported.

### facade-top-pyo3 — refuted leads

- **Runtime licensing vs generated table / hand-written allow path**: `support::classify` reads only `NA_RULES` and `LICENSED` from the generated `support_matrix_data.rs`, falls through to `CellStatus::Refused`. The only non-table success path is `ALLOWED_RULES`, which is generated and empty (`support_matrix_data.rs:426-428`), and closed rules are checked *before* it. `effective_graph_class` relabels only a bidirected-free ADMG as DAG under `AverageEffect` (mathematically the same object); CPDAG/PAG never collapse. Sound, except for the off-axis skip (finding 8).
- **Refusal reason codes from the closed registry**: `unsupported_reason!`/`support_reason!`/`compile_reason!` const-assert `is_runtime_refusal_code` at compile time (doc-tested with `compile_fail`); `not_identified` has a module-level const assert; the binding uses `reason_code!` for its own codes. Un-coded `Unsupported { message }` literals exist but get the registered default `route_not_supported` in `py_err`. Fine.
- **`identify_api.rs:431` `_ => return Ok(())`**: legitimately N/A. The audit re-identifies at each *additional* horizon; only `Response` (temporal) and `Mediation` carry a horizon list. `TemporalEffect` has a single `horizon_steps`, which is the witness itself, and no other query kind reaches the temporal-class branch (`temporal_class_witness` errors first, `:380`). Minor: `horizons.first().unwrap_or(1)` (`:370,:377`) would silently identify at h=1 for an empty list; relies on upstream non-empty validation.
- **`estimator_config.rs` unknown keys / default drift**: every top-level key is validated against the per-estimator table before extraction (`:232-259`), with a "belongs to estimator X" hint; `overlap` and `glm_options` sub-keys are validated too; the final `other =>` arm errors. All estimators are built from `X::new()` plus explicit overrides only, so Python and Rust defaults are the same objects. No hidden defaults found.
- **Buffer protocol (`lib.rs:2120-2175`)**: `PosteriorArtifact` has no setters, no `&mut self` methods and is never `borrow_mut`ed, so `draws` is immutable for the object's life; `view.obj` is INCREF'd, keeping the owner (and the `Vec`) alive for every exported view; WRITABLE is refused and `readonly=1`; `len = n*8`, `itemsize=8`, `format="d"` only under `PyBUF_FORMAT`, shape/strides boxed only under ND/STRIDES and freed with the matching `Box<[isize;1]>`/`CString::from_raw` in `__releasebuffer__`. Correct. Only gap: no `// SAFETY:` comment stating the immutability invariant — add one and a `#[pyclass(frozen)]` to make it compiler-enforced.
- **Arrow capsule import (`lib.rs:2292-2330`)**: capsule names are checked (`pointer_checked(Some(c"arrow_schema"/"arrow_array"))`), null-checked, structs are moved out with `ptr::read` and replaced by `empty()` (release = NULL) so the producer's capsule destructor is a no-op — the standard CDI move idiom; release is called once by the Rust owner. OK. The `// SAFETY` on `from_ffi` ("compliant exporter") is an assumption about foreign code, which is inherent to CDI.
- **`.expect`/`unwrap`/`unreachable!` reachable from Python input**: `u32::try_from(i).expect` sites need >2³² nodes/variables (construction already caps at u32). `transport_grid_api.rs:175` re-parses a string produced by `serde_json::json!(..).to_string()` three lines earlier (NaN is serialised as `null`), so it cannot fail. The `unreachable!`s follow an explicit match on the same value (`temporal_api.rs:1364` algo string validated by caller; `response_api.rs:346` "checked above"; `prepared_api.rs:3498` guarded by `mediation.is_some() || counterfactual.is_some()`). `observation_api.rs:256` `.expect("response route")` holds because the query is always `Response`. Also: no `panic = "abort"` profile, and entry points go through `catch_ffi`/`detach_catch`, so a panic becomes `CausalError("internal Rust panic: ..")`, not a process abort. (pymethods not wrapped in `catch_ffi` would surface PyO3's `PanicException`, a `BaseException`; none of the sites above is realistically reachable.)
- **`prepared_api.rs` is a copy of `analysis/prepared.rs`**: no — it is 25 `prepare_*` entry points of argument parsing + builder calls that delegate to `Study…build().prepare()`. The real duplication is confined to `composite_result_wire`/`export_artifact` (finding 4).
- **GIL**: all long native paths I checked (`ate_api`, `prepared_api`, `discovery_api`, `temporal_api`, `gcm_api`, `attribution_api`, `stability`, `bayesian`, transport) run under `py.detach`; callbacks re-acquire with `Python::attach` and discovery forces `threads=1` when a Python CI is used. `design_api::rank_designs` holds the GIL (`catch_ffi` only) — acceptable since its utility is a Python callback.
- **Error taxonomy in the binding**: distinct Python classes per kind, reason codes preserved via `split_prefix`, estimator `Refused`/`TargetPopulation` routed to the unsupported class, `CacheBudget` to `CausalResourceError`. The only flattening is finding 1.

### facade-top-pyo3 — reviewed and sound

- `support::classify` / `refuse_if_not_applicable` / `refusal_reason` ordering (n/a → licensed → closed-rule text → (empty) allowlist → default refusal); `query_axis_name` Dynamic-policy classification by schedule length.
- `audit_temporal_class_horizons`: per-horizon re-identification, case matching by completion fingerprint, downgrade to `GraphDependent` with estimands cleared when any horizon fails, `truncated_completions` max-merge and forced `PartiallyIdentified` + `invariant=None` when enumeration was capped.
- `reported_se_interval_z`: `0.5 + 0.95/2 == 0.975` exactly in binary64; one quantile owner.
- `primary_interval_binding` precedence (response uncertainty → bounded distribution interval → posterior → SE → identified set) and `reported_interval_bindings` de-duplication by method.
- `error.rs` reason-code macros (compile-time registry check) and `CausalError::not_identified` (capped search is stated as "not a proof of non-identification").

- `parse_estimator_config` key validation and `merge_rd_triple` conflict refusal.

### identify-a — refuted leads

- **Memo key omits the current distribution** — refuted. `SubproblemKey` (`id.rs:36-42`) includes `dist: DistCtx` and `assign`; `DistCtx` derives Hash/Eq over sumset + frozen factor list. `PreparedAdmg::ancestor_memo` keys on (seeds ∩ active, active).
- **Line 6/7 conditioning sets / wrong topological order** — refuted for the cases the code accepts. Marginal mode conditions on all π-predecessors within the current `v` (chain rule of the marginal `P(v)`; valid because line 2 only ever shrinks `v` to an ancestral set while in Marginal mode). Line 7 freezes `P(v_i | all π-predecessors in the pre-line-7 v)`, which is exactly `v_π^{(i-1)} ∩ S'` as variables plus `v_π^{(i-1)} \ S'` as fixed values. With an empty sumset the telescope argument holds (each frozen factor is a conditional of its own variable and mentions only earlier variables), so line-6 and nested line-7 selection by filtering is exact. One global π is used for all subgraphs, which is consistent. Front-door trace reproduces `sum_m P(m|t) sum_t' P(y|t',m) P(t')`; bound-vs-free T is handled by `effective_assign` (`id.rs:907-908`).
- **Hedge (F, F') not a hedge for the original query** — refuted: emits (current G, S) exactly as SP line 5 `FAIL(G, S)`; by SP Thm 4 that witnesses non-ID of the original query via an ancestral sub-query. The certificate does not name the sub-query's (X', Y'), which would be a nice addition.
- **IDC rule-2 graph / ratio** — refuted. `mutilate_bar_x_underline_z` drops directed edges into X, bidirected edges incident to X, and directed edges out of Z while keeping bidirected edges at Z; the test conditions on `X ∪ Z\{z}`; final `P'/sum_y P'` is right. The `Int64(0)` placeholder for moved Z is harmless because Distribution queries run with an empty `Assign`. (IDC does not call `validate()`, see identify-a-8.)
- **O-set formula / optimality wording** — refuted. `efficient.rs:354-387` computes `pa(cn) \ (de(cn) ∪ {T})` with `descendants_of` including seeds (verified). The doc does carry the "fully observed DAG" qualifier (`efficient.rs:7-8,352`), and the result holds beyond linear models (Rotnitzky & Smucler 2020), so no linear-model qualifier is needed. The O-set is still validated against the back-door criterion and the fallback is used when it contains a forbidden variable.
- **`frontdoor.rs:317 combinations` duplicate** — it is a second copy of `enum_masks::for_each_mask_of_size` (materialising instead of streaming); DRY only, no drift. Front-door conditions (i) interception via node removal + reachability, (ii) T ⊥ M in G with T's out-edges cut, (iii) M ⊥ Y | T in G with M's out-edges cut, are all correct.
- **IV mutilated graph** — refuted. Exogeneity+exclusion are tested as Z ⊥ Y in G with all of T's out-edges removed (total-effect version), Z ∉ De(T), relevance by d-connection; status is `IdentifiedUnderParametricRestrictions` and `ExclusionRestriction` is recorded (until Auto drops it, identify-a-3).
- **`bounds.rs:191` partial_cmp** — refuted: inputs are validated finite and the matrix is 0/1, so no NaN reaches the comparator. Basis enumeration over C(16,7) with rank-7 system (3 cells per arm + normalisation) is a correct vertex enumeration for a bounded LP; duplicate columns (never-/always-taker types with an unobserved potential outcome) give singular bases that are skipped, and every BFS is still reached through another basis. Pinned against bpbounds Table 1.
- **`intervention_support.rs:274 _ => Ok(None)`** — refuted: it is `bernoulli_weight` answering "is this a Bernoulli policy?"; non-Bernoulli interventions continue to `normalize_to_set`, which refuses everything unsupported.
- **`u32::try_from(..).expect` sites** — refuted as user-reachable: node ids are `u32` by construction, so `node_count() <= u32::MAX`.
- **Auto turns a failed strategy into a negative claim** — partly refuted: errors become `auto.<method>.error` Execution/Warning diagnostics, which is the convention `IdentificationStatus::NotIdentified` documents. The real defects on that path are identify-a-3/-6/-12.
- **`response_id.rs:3` vs `joint_response.rs:5`** — both statements are about different code: joint_response (multi-treatment adjustment) is honestly "sufficient, not complete"; response_id's "complete" is wrong twice over (identify-a-1, identify-a-2).
- **Temporal lag off-by-one** — none found. `lag = reference − offset`, parents located at `offset + child_lag − parent_lag`, window = `history` slices back with growth until no ancestor of {T…,Y} sits on the deepest slice with a truncatable variable; at that fixed point An({T,Y}) is fully inside the window, minimal separators lie inside An({T,Y}) (so the default `minimal_only` search is exact), and general ID's line 2 restricts to An(Y). Caveat: exactness relies on `minimal_only = true`; with `maximal_only` a non-ancestor candidate at the window edge could be accepted on a truncated graph.

### identify-a — reviewed and sound

- `id.rs` lines 1–5 mapping, line 6 (Marginal and empty-sumset CFactor), line 7 C-factor freezing, line 1 with carried sumset, memoisation, two-sided ATE/schedule contrast assembly.
- `idc.rs` full algorithm.
- `backdoor.rs` criterion (De(T) forbidden, d-sep in G with T's out-edges removed), size-ordered minimal enumeration with superset pruning, An({T,Y}) screen, O-set fallback with validation, budget diagnostic.
- `efficient.rs` O-set and min-cardinality fallback.
- `generalized.rs` MAG amenability via visibility, proper back-door graph (only first edges of proper causal paths removed), Forb = De(cn), candidate family An ∪ De(T) minus Forb; envelope downgrades on completion cap / skipped class audit; conditional (effect-modifier) check requires pre-treatment W and Z ∪ W separation.
- `joint_response.rs` `visible()` against Zhang's definition (direct witness and collider-path witness with internal nodes parents of B); Def. 20 check.
- `frontdoor.rs`, `iv.rs` criteria; `bounds.rs` LP.
- `path_specific.rs` recanting-witness reduction to "a treatment child starts both a selected and a complementary path" (proved both directions), edge g-formula on V* = An(Y) not through T with refusal on any bidirected edge in V*, fail-closed on truncated enumeration (modulo identify-a-6b), Pearl NDE/NIE orientation.
- `tiered.rs` closure argument on the CoDetermined ADMG (every back-door path meets a conditioned non-collider).
- `temporal_backdoor.rs` window growth / fixed point, parent-adjustment fallback (pa(T) is a valid back-door set under sufficiency; refuses when Y ∈ pa(T) or a parent exceeds `max_history_lag`), history-lag filter.
- `temporal_mag.rs` definite/possible-parent closure and conservative visibility under truncation; `temporal_mediation.rs` shared-set certification (T→M, joint (T,M)→Y, T→Y) with parametric status and explicit alias assumption.
- `envelope.rs` mass accounting and status lattice.

Not reviewed line-by-line (time): `temporal_generalized.rs`, `identifier.rs`, tests in each file, `assumptions.rs` (20 lines, trivial).

### identify-sid — refuted leads

- "Lines 5, 6, 9, 11 are missing from `Rule::name`": refuted. In Figure 5 lines 5, 6, 9 are pure
  conditions (`if C(D\X)={C0}`, `if C(D)≠{D}`, `else`) and 11 is FAIL; they are `districts.len()==1`
  (:738), `containing.len()==1` (:753) and `return Ok(None)` + obstruction (:754-759). Branch order equals
  the published order 1→2→3→4→(6→7→8)→10→11. The two-pass structure (pure ID first, then with line 10)
  does not change completeness: pass 2 is exactly Figure 5.
- "Enlarge weight may be un-normalised": refuted (see -1): normalised for P*, its marginals, and
  Q-factors at every external value; zero weight × undefined child is handled by the evaluator. The
  real issue is the support requirement.
- "d-separation in the wrong graph": refuted. `admissible_selections` builds the induced subgraph on
  state.v, drops directed edges into X and bidirected edges touching X, adds one parentless node per
  selection target inside v\X, conditions on X (∪ Z). One node per target is equivalent to the paper's S
  nodes because S is only ever a path endpoint. Restricting to the induced subgraph (dropping S nodes on
  removed non-ancestors / other districts) is what Figure 5 does (D := An(Y)_D, D := C'); I checked that
  any path through a removed node contains an unactivated collider or an intervened node.
  `source_from`'s extra do(external parents) is rule-3-sound in both populations.
- "Memo key omits population/kernel": refuted. Key = (y, x, v, kernel ExprId, source flag); sources are
  fixed per engine and the memo is cleared when `source_catalog` changes (:1770, meta.rs:332).
- "Budget exhaustion could become a negative": refuted for identification: `charge` → Err on every solve
  path and in the verifier; tests pin it. Only nuance: `negative_witness` (:1494-1497) and meta.rs:301 map
  a verifier Err (incl. memory budget) to `NotCertified` — weaker, never stronger.
- "Tampered record can pass": refuted. Root state must equal a freshly interned initial state, each
  child state must equal the recomputed successor, outputs are compared by hash-consed id, Source leaves
  field-wise with NaN-symbolic values required, Standardize only at the root and never in meta, children
  indices strictly decreasing, all ids range-checked before indexing.
- "ProvenNonTransportable emitted when merely not identified": refuted. It is emitted only after
  `verify_s_hedge`, which checks every clause of Definition 10: both forests are edge subgraphs, ≤1
  child per node, single bidirected component spanning all nodes, equal root sets, F∩X≠∅, F'∩X=∅,
  F'⊆F, R⊂An(Y)_{G_X̄}, and a selection target inside F' (footnote 10 / Def. 9 "any vertex, not
  necessarily root"). Using the full X and Y is stricter than Corollary 4's X'⊆X (take X'=X∩F;
  An(Y)_{G_X̄} ⊆ An(Y)_{G_X̄'}). Meta negatives require the same forests to verify under every source.
  Witness construction failure degrades to NotCertified.
- bind: `bind_distribution` requires same population, Available, Joint, no restricted values, no
  regime conditioning, EXACT intervention set, vars∪conditions ⊆ measured; failure is
  `transport.missing_evidence`, and catalog search returns MissingEvidence/NotCertified, never a negative.
- legacy `s_admissible_standardizers` not conditioning on X: sound and equivalent (X has no incoming
  edges in D_{X̄}, so it is never a collider/descendant; conditioning on it can only block).
- legacy Direct rule with bidirected edges: exact — with only X conditioned and X parentless in D_{X̄}
  no collider is ever activated, so S is connected to Y iff a directed path avoiding X exists, which is
  what `outcome_ancestors` computes.

### io — refuted leads

- `graph_networkx.rs:217-223 index.get(&from_name).unwrap()` — safe: the first loop interns every `n.id` via the same `json_id_to_string`, and `intern` is idempotent; mixed int/str ids merge (io-17) but cannot miss.
- `graph_dot.rs:156,168` / `graph_mixed.rs:1238` `names.expect("checked")` — guarded by `use_names = names.is_some_and(len == node_count)`; unreachable panic.
- PAG circle marks lost on export — refuted for this crate's exporters: `pag_to_dot` writes `mark_a/mark_b` for every non-(tail/arrow) combination (graph_mixed.rs:363-371), GML carries `marked` edges, and static `Pag` has no middle marks (antecedent-graph pag.rs:155,175 always `Empty`); temporal/LPCMCI middle marks are kept in identity (identity.rs:546-590). The loss is on *import* by the DAG reader / foreign spellings (io-3).
- `validate_uncertainty` duplicated in `crates/antecedent/src/analysis/statistical.rs:1341` — same name, unrelated function (that one re-verifies a statistical-transport bootstrap row: replicate accounting `ok+failed==requested`, seed, percentile interval recomputation). No duplication.
- mmap alignment for f64 views — no typed views exist; `MappedSection` exposes `&[u8]` only and posterior draws are copied via `f64::from_le_bytes` (posterior.rs:253-258). The uncompressed mapped path is bounds-checked with `checked_add` (reader.rs:365-371). Only the compressed path is not (io-6).
- `transport_certificate.rs` `not_certified` literal vs registry code — `check()` recomputes the whole reasoning section from the outcome and requires equality (:164), positive proofs are re-checked and negative s-hedges re-verified against every source (:222-236); a `NotCertified` outcome cannot be upgraded by editing without failing proof check. The literal is a slot status, not a registry reason code. Sound.
- `migrate.rs:230` — a 0.2 `transport_identification` NotCertified section passes through byte-identical; migration never rewrites non-schema sections, and the 0.1 path now errors on manifest/section count mismatch. Cannot produce identified/licensed output from a refused one. (The only fabrication in migration is io-13.)
- Identity canonicalisation: digests are over CBOR of structs (declaration-order maps, no HashMap in any hashed wire — checked by grep), floats as `to_bits` u64 or ciborium shortest-exact (injective, −0.0/NaN payload preserved), set-valued fields sorted+deduped, domain-separated derive-keys with a format prefix, 32-byte digests untruncated. Bootstrap count, seeds, confidence level (response), clip/trim/caliper, learner configs, snapshot and target-weights are all present in `InferenceBindingWire`/`EstimatorConfigWire`/`ExecutionIdentityWire`. I did not find a science-changing field missing from the wires (whether the facade populates every one is outside this unit).
- `contract_section.rs` 212-line `contract_for` is test-only fixture code (inside `mod tests`), not a production function.

### io — reviewed and sound

- Container framing (container.rs): header/manifest size caps, per-section length == manifest `compressed_size`, BLAKE3 over on-wire bytes before decompress, logical length check, write-side re-verification of cached zstd buffers, streaming skip with hash.
- `expr_arena_from_wire` (expr_wire.rs:204-293): canonical var-set ordering, duplicate-table-entry detection via re-interning index equality, child < index acyclicity for every node kind, derivation parents < id, unique derivation ids.
- `verify_contract_against_body` / `verify_stored_payloads` / `verify_layer_links` / score-reuse and target-weights links (contract_section.rs:480-903): every advertised digest rehashed, nested digests cross-checked, seal and claim id recomputed, claim value bits vs body, domains and kind re-derived, calibration slot re-derived from basis and basis tied to rehashed payloads; unverified -> storage-only operations.
- `validate_response_value` / `validate_response_estimate` / `validate_support` (causal_artifact.rs:518-1075): shapes, ordered envelopes, finiteness, status-payload agreement, duplicate graph ids.
- `identified_set_interval_from_wire` (analysis_result_artifact.rs:196-245).
- `LearnedTrialWire::verify` (learned_trial_wire.rs:33-110): re-identifies, replays the AIPW score from retained nuisances with exact equality, replicate accounting `len + failures == bootstrap`, strictly increasing replicate ids, percentile interval recomputed and compared, reason code re-derived.
- `TransportCertificateWire::check` and `EvidenceCatalogWire::to_catalog` tag/domain decoding.
- `validate_posterior_meta` shape/finite/ordering/mass checks (modulo io-12).
- `PayloadDigestWire` encodings (length-prefixed kind label; length-prefixed groups — no concatenation ambiguity).

### model-learn-cf — refuted leads

- **`lgssm.rs:25` "cache key collides two f64 tolerances"** — it is not a cache key; it is innovation bit-packing (see finding 20 for the real problem).
- **Kalman/RTS equations, Joseph form, symmetry, log-likelihood constant** — scalar model, so symmetry/Joseph form are moot; `p = (pp/s)·r` is the cancellation-free form of `(1−k)pp`; RTS gain `J = P_f a / P_pred`, smoothed variance and lag-one covariance `J_{t}·P_s[t+1]` are correct; FFBS conditional variance `P_f·q/P_pred` equals `P_f − J²P_pred`; prediction-error log-likelihood includes `−½ln 2π − ½ln S − ln(scale)` correctly; initial variance `p0 = q` matches the generative `x0 = μ0 + σ_p ε`. EM M-steps for a, q, r are the standard Shumway–Stoffer updates (only caveat: fixed 25 iterations, no convergence report).
- **`registry.rs:2583` test "asserting little"** — it compares per-row log-probabilities of two independently parameterised fits to 1e-8, weighted and unweighted; it can fail and tests the right invariance.
- **`registry.rs:371` failures collected as strings** — they are surfaced in `MechanismAssignment::failed_families` and in the "no candidates" error; losing the typed error is cosmetic. The real defect is finding 1.
- **`gaussian-process` "never built, may not compile"** — `scripts/gate_gcm.sh:118` runs `cargo test -p antecedent-model --features gaussian-process`; by inspection the cfg'd items (`cholesky_spd`, `chol_solve`, `chol_log_det` import at :28, fit at :1669) are consistent. Not in GitHub CI though (finding 26).
- **`gbt.rs:80` `learning_rate as f32`** — forust's API is f32; harmless (identity string keeps the f64 bits, the model uses the nearest f32).
- **rand 0.8 vs 0.9** — 0.8 comes from burn-*, forust-ml, smartcore, rand_distr 0.4, num-complex; 0.9 from faer, candle-core, half, rand_distr 0.5. None of our code samples through either: all seeded paths use `antecedent_core::CausalRng` and hand a `u64` to the provider (`ctx.rng.stream(1|2|0x4E455500).next_u64()`). Nothing claims equal streams across learners. No issue.
- **Forest determinism** — smartcore seeds one `StdRng` from the supplied seed for bootstrap and feature sampling; single-threaded; deterministic. Portable export key `forest_regressor` matches smartcore 0.4.10 for both RF and ExtraTrees; `<=` split matches `inclusive: true`.
- **Cross-fit leakage** — transformers are fit on the training rows only (`tf.fit(x, RowSelection(train))`); Auto selection runs inside each fold's training rows (`AutoLearner::fit` materialises the view first; test `selection_cannot_read_rows_outside_training_view` is meaningful). Elastic-net standardisation happens inside `fit`, i.e. per training fold. No leakage found.
- **Elastic-net back-transform** — β = β_std/scale, b0 = ȳ − Σ x̄_c β_c; soft-threshold update `S(ρ, λα)/(‖x‖² + λ(1−α))` is correct for the stated (un-normalised) objective.
- **Additive-noise invertibility** — LinearGaussian / LinearBasis / GP / Bvar / Hierarchical abduction is exact `y − f(pa)`; no post-nonlinear family exists; non-invertible families are tagged `Posterior` rather than approximated (but see findings 11, 12, 21).
- **burn "GPU numerically equivalent" claim** — none found; README says CPU NdArray. Inference re-implements the forward pass with the correct `[d_in, d_out]` weight layout.
- **McmcDoSampler** — its doc comment is candid that it targets a KDE proxy of the outcome margin; variance inflation by h² (21 % at 64 pilots, 7 % at 1000) is inherent and disclosed in spirit. Could add the number to `notes`.

### model-learn-cf — reviewed and sound

- `lgssm.rs`: `kalman_filter`, `rts_smooth`, FFBS in `infer_lgssm_innovations`, unit rescaling (`scaled_lgssm`), log-likelihood in `log_prob_column`.
- `registry.rs`: multinomial-logit standardise/back-transform (:713-725); `lgssm_em_normalized` M-step; `solve_scaled_ridge` change of variables; `basis_cv_mse`/`basis_cv_loglik` fold hygiene (train-only fit, skipped folds handled, non-convergence is an error); `discrete_support` weighting; GP NLML (`½yᵀα + ½log|K| + n/2·log 2π`) and dual-form mean.
- `mechanism.rs`: Gaussian log-densities; both softmax implementations (max-shifted); inverse-CDF bin construction (apart from the ε edge case); `evaluate_batch_topo` ordering.
- `engine.rs`: abduction order (topological, prior-imputed parents propagated before child inversion), shape validation, outcomes-only retention indexing, column-frozen nested pass vs per-unit reference, refusal for row-coupled nested queries.
- `crossfit.rs`: OOF assembly, `cross_fit_selected` eligibility logic, `diagnose` (RMSE/R²/log-loss with clipping).
- `portable.rs`: validation (forward-only child links ⇒ termination), NaN routing, sum vs vote-mean aggregation, non-finite output refusal; forust and smartcore exports match the providers' split conventions.
- `auto.rs`: candidate construction and nested-selection isolation.
- `do_sampler.rs`: kernel/density IPW weight form, Kish ESS, MH acceptance with cached density.
- `evaluate.rs`: local-Markov comparison set (topologically earlier non-parents ⊂ non-descendants), MC p-value `(1+count)/(1+n)`.

### prob — refuted leads

- `mcmc_stats.rs:254,269,296` NaN-tolerant sorts: `has_variation(&raw)` (:206, :236-250) rejects any non-finite value before any sort; folded values and indicators are derived from finite inputs. No NaN reaches the comparators. (Not true of `posterior.rs:197` — see prob-5.)
- `mcmc_stats` R-hat / ESS version: it is rank-normalised split-R̂ and folded split-R̂ (max of both), ranks pooled over split chains with average ties, Blom offsets (r−3/8)/(S+1/4), bulk ESS on z-scores, tail ESS = min over 5%/95% indicators, Geyer initial-positive + monotone sequence, ρ̂ₜ = 1 − (W − āₜ)/var⁺, τ floor 1/log10(S) — matches Vehtari et al. (2021)/Stan. Deviations are immaterial: lag autocovariances divide by (n−1) not n (factor n/(n−1)); odd draw counts drop the last draw rather than the middle; Stan's final odd-lag correction term is omitted; type-7 quantiles for the tail cut. 1 chain → `fail` (R̂=NaN→∞, ESS 0): conservative (the `ess_guard` in `mcmc_summary` that seems to allow 1 chain is moot). Constant chains → NaN/0/∞, fail closed. NaN cannot enter `tau` because inputs are z-scores/indicators with var⁺>0.
- `external_prior.rs:340,377,400,456` `unwrap_or(0.0)` on mixture weight: `validate_mixture_weights` enforces all-or-none before either path runs, so the default is unreachable on the mixture path. Power-add and moment-matched mixture formulas are correct; `composed_ess` is withheld on the mixture path and when any contributing source lacks ess.
- `kish_ess` (:40): correct `(Σw)²/Σw²`; NaN weights give 0.0.
- `graph_samples.rs:124` `w/total`: `validate_weights` (finite, ≥0, total finite and >0) runs immediately before.
- `likelihood_terms.rs:91` `log_normal_cdf`: checked x ∈ {−40,−38,−30,−20,−10,−8.0001,−8,−7.9999,−5,−1,0,1,5,8,10} against erfc and a 20000-term continued fraction: relative error ≤1.2e-16 everywhere; 64-term CF is converged at t=8 (δ identical to 16 digits); probit curvature is continuous across the x=−8 switch (0.98567478736336 vs 0.98567478736348).
- Gamma / inverse-gamma samplers: Marsaglia–Tsang constants and squeeze are right; shape<1 boost `G(a+1)·U^{1/a}` is right; `rate` is a rate (`d·v/rate`); `InvGamma(shape, scale) = 1/Gamma(shape, rate=scale)` is the correct convention. NIG update formulas (Vn, mn, αn = α0+Σw/2, βn) are algebraically correct — the defect is numerical (prob-1).
- HMC leapfrog: half-step p, (full q, full p)×(L−1), full q, half p — correct and reversible; mass enters momentum draw (√m·z), kinetic energy (p²/2m) and position update (p/m) consistently; ΔH>1000 or non-finite → divergent, rejected, counted separately for warm-up and post-warm-up; any post-warm-up divergence refuses publication; warm-up draws are not stored; Gaussian joint target `−Aλ − B(β)e^{−λ}` with A = a0+(n+p)/2 includes the log-σ² Jacobian correctly.
- Laplace: neg-Hessian sign, prior curvature, Newton direction, backtracking, and the "stalled line search ⇒ Newton decrement below objective round-off" convergence rule are sound (decrement is scale-free: at n=5000 it bounds the mode error at ~1e-5 posterior SDs). Non-PD Hessian is not silently regularised (it errors — see prob-7). Non-convergence is refused.

- `mcmc_stats.rs` in full (rank normalisation, folding, split stats, lazy Geyer ESS, guards).
- `hmc.rs` transitions (`hmc_step_target`, `hmc_step_glm`), `finalize_energy`, `TransitionStats`, packing/gating; `diagnostics.rs::mcmc_publication_ok` (R̂≤1.01, ESS ≥100·chains, zero divergences, moved, ≥2 chains).
- `gaussian_target.rs` both targets (density, gradient, Jacobian).
- `likelihood_terms.rs` logit (stable tails), probit (Mills CF), Poisson (overflow → Numerical → divergence), zero-weight row handling, `validate_design`.
- `conjugate.rs` known-σ² and NIG algebra, MVN/NIG samplers, gamma sampler.
- `conjugate_moment_match.rs` Beta/Gamma moment and mean+ESS constructors (exact; note `ess()` can be negative for α+β<2, by the documented convention).
- `external_prior.rs` power-add and mixture moment matching, ESS bookkeeping.
- `laplace.rs` Newton/line-search loop, known-σ² closed form, `sample_gaussian_mvn`.

### python-pkg — refuted leads

- **Python computes intervals/p-values/significance (`est ± 1.96*se` etc.)**: none found. No `1.96`, `ppf`, `sqrt`, quantile, or alpha comparison anywhere in the package; HTML/repr print native `q025/q975`, `mean_interval`, and SE verbatim. The real problems are slot/verdict re-derivation (findings 1, 4, 7), not arithmetic.
- **`_data.py:84/103/124` masks Arrow errors and changes NaN semantics**: the swallowing is real but harmless for missingness: Rust maps NaN to null on the numpy path (`python/src/lib.rs:2216-2219`) and unifies NaN/validity on the Arrow path (`crates/antecedent-data/src/column.rs:300-360`), so both ingest paths agree on what is missing. The dtype difference is finding 15.
- **`_api.py:112,119`**: best-effort name lookup on the error path only, used to label pending edges in a refusal that is re-raised unchanged. No status depends on it.
- **`results/_html.py` x8**: each falls back to `repr(self)`, which is itself limitation-aware (withholds the point when `rendering_limitation()` is set). No status is hidden. Under `ANTECEDENT_STRICT_ANSWER=1` the HTML falls back to repr rather than raising, which is acceptable.
- **`graph.py:102`**: fail-closed (re-raises as `CausalValueError`).
- **`accepted_graph.py:64` `except ValueError: pass`**: intended fallthrough from "is it a DAG" to CPDAG/PAG construction, which refuses unknown marks at :91-96. Sound (but see findings 9, 10).
- **`accepted_graph.py:347,368` "proves"/"guarantees"**: accurate in context (no undirected edges remain when `new_undirected` is empty; `try_into_temporal_dag` success means nothing pending). `:780` is fail-closed.
- **Python defaults vs Rust defaults on the main path**: `analyze`/`prepare` forward `None` and read `omitted_defaults()` from Rust (`_defaults.py`, `python/src/lib.rs:2496-2513`). Exceptions are findings 12-13.
- **Seeds**: no `None -> nondeterministic` path. Defaults are a fixed `seed=1` (151 sites); `estimate/refresh(seed=None)` means "the seed given at prepare". Native `.pyi` stubs show `seed=0` on some low-level functions, but the Python wrappers always pass their own. `crossfit_seed` is recorded on `EstimateView`.
- **`gcm.py:138` / `accepted_graph.py:936` `__all__`**: the omitted names are imports used internally, not re-exports; `fit_gcm_discovered` correctly takes `names` from the data, so isolated nodes survive there.
- **Result mutability**: views are frozen pydantic/dataclasses with mutable interior lists/dicts. For native results `export()` reads `_execution`, so mutation cannot change the artifact. Only transport views are affected (finding 2).
- **`_verdict.py`**: exact-string lookup, unknown status warns and closes as "not identified". Sound.

- `cov.classical_transport.*` `dgp` pointers do not resolve -> they resolve now (four functions exist in `v20_transport_statistical_calibration.rs`). 197 naive non-matches are all imports or macro-generated tests the schema gate's resolver handles.

- `max_uses = 170` is not a ratchet -> it is (3 slack, lowering is free, raising is a reviewed edit); the concern is magnitude, not mechanism.

- `implementation_exists` used on licensed cells -> none.

### scripts-ci — refuted leads

- **"cargo test filters that match zero tests"**: none today. Every literal filter in every gate and all 265 gate_calibration.sh names resolve to a test at f5660026 (list in scripts-ci-4). The defect is the absence of a count check, not a currently empty filter.
- **Regex TOML parser as a live fail-open**: refuted as fail-open, confirmed as duplication. gate_parity_schema.sh:326-341 compares the regex result with `tomllib` for id and status on every enrolled manifest, new manifests must enrol (:135-140), and every feature gate calls it first. gate_composition.sh no longer contains a copy.
- **Static Rust resolver can be satisfied by `#[cfg(any())]`, commented-out, non-compiled, macro-generated or `#[ignore]`d tests**: refuted for `test_evidence.py`. `mask_rust` blanks comments and string contents (nested block comments, raw strings, char literals handled); `_disabling_cfg` rejects any `#[cfg(...)]` other than `cfg(test)` on the fn, on every enclosing `mod {}` block, on the `mod x;` declaration and as an inner `#![cfg]`; `compiled()` walks `mod` declarations from the target root; macro-generated names have no `fn <name>` and fail closed; and `resolve_rust_test` additionally requires the module-qualified name in `cargo test -- --list` and not in `--list --ignored`. The weaker hand regex in gate_parity_schema.sh:300-319 is the real gap (scripts-ci-6).
- **`if False:` Python tests**: refuted; a nested `def` has indentation and is rejected as "not a module-level test function". `skipif` on one line is caught (`\bskip`). Module-level `importorskip` fails closed for evidence rows because `--collect-only -q` prints no node id.
- **"22 `allow` lines with no justification"**: there are 23, and each group carries a comment stating why the reference cannot run facet code (dispatch arms, type-only result slots, error `From` impls, `pub mod`). The text is free-form and unenforced, but it is present; `check()` fails on any stale or missing name, so the list cannot rot silently.
- **Attestation misses renames / files moved out of a facet / new uncovered files**: refuted. A renamed or deleted path shows up in `git diff --name-only <sha> -- <paths>` under its old name and maps to the covering directory's facet (normally `core`, which every record carries); new files under a listed `src/` directory are `core` automatically; a new crate, build.rs, suite using `for_record(` or tests/common file outside the list fails `check`. Untracked files are included (`ls-files --others`). Cargo.toml/Cargo.lock normalisation strips only workspace version fields and `antecedent*` lock entries; external version bumps, features and profiles still drift `core`.
- **Collector acceptance band with an absolute-tolerance clause**: there is none. The collector makes no pass/fail decision; the harness does (`level +- 3*MCSE`, recheck below `level - 0.02`, floor `level - 2*MCSE` from 1000 replicates), and gate_parity_schema.sh:594-667 re-derives the same band per grid point from the stored numbers, so a hand-edited `observed` outside the band fails. Skipped replicates are capped at 5 % and failures to report count as misses. The hole is the role that bypasses the recheck (scripts-ci-2), plus named boundaries at non-base grid points being "recorded, not gated" (they are labelled boundary, so they never read as calibrated).
- **`trap ... RETURN` under `set -e`**: harmless here. Each of the three uses is in a `self_test` that is followed immediately by `exit $?`; bash does not run a RETURN trap for nested function calls without `functrace`.
- **GNU vs BSD tool reliance**: none found. No `grep -P`, `sed -i`, `readlink -f`, `date -d`, `mapfile`, `declare -A`; gate_transport.sh uses `sed -E` with portable scripts; heavy lifting is in python3.
- **`pull_request_target` / secret exposure to forks**: not used. All workflows default to `permissions: contents: read`; `id-token: write` and `contents: write` are scoped to the two publishing jobs.
- **Feature gates lacking uv hard-fail vs gate_composition**: confirmed as stated (folded into scripts-ci-7), not refuted.

- `scripts/test_evidence.py` Rust lexer/masker, attribute walk-back, `target_modules` compilation-unit walk, `module_path`, closure construction and fixture-consumption rule (names `conformance/<cat>/<name>` in code, parses, asserts).
- `scripts/collect_coverage_records.py` `record_lines` / `merge_grid`: recheck log replaces the first run; all three grid points required; non-point fields must agree across points; strictly increasing n; governing point = lowest failing, else lowest; smoke lines refused. gate_parity_schema.sh re-derives all of it.
- `scripts/calibration_facets.py`: `load_surface` (longest-prefix facet), `check` completeness list, `_normalized_manifest`, `changed_paths`, `waiver_applies` (from == record sha, drifted-relevant subset of waiver paths, nothing relevant changed after `to`), `validate_waivers` (paths change between from/to, every path exercised by a replay record that carries the facet and can reach the file, fingerprints over exact float bits), `attestation_verdict` (empty registry, unresolvable sha and any invalid waiver all fail).
