### stats-a — refuted leads

- `cluster_meat` does not demean scores: correct for regression/GLM scores — X'e = 0 (resp. X'u = 0) at the fitted coefficients, so the score sum is already zero; demeaning per column would be wrong when there is no intercept. The scalar IF family must demean (E psi != 0) and does. Finite-sample factors agree: (G/(G-1))((n-1)/(n-p)) reduces to G/(G-1) at p = 1. Each is right for its use.
- HC0–HC3 formulas, HC1 factor n/(n-p), and the GLM convention: bread (X'WX)^-1, meat sum u_i^2 x x' with u = (y-mu) mu'(eta)/V, leverage h = w x'(X'WX)^-1 x — all correct and match `glm_adjustment.rs::glm_score_components`. Pinned 4-row values are consistent with hand computation.
- `hat.as_ref().unwrap()`: `hat` is `Some` for exactly the HC2/HC3 arms; unreachable panic.
- Newey–West: Bartlett 1 - l/(L+1), Gamma_l + Gamma_l', lag capped at T-1, no small-sample factor (statsmodels default). Correct.
- Multiway finite-sample factor per subset with its own G (CGM) and G<2 error per subset: correct.
- Poisson / NB2 / Bernoulli deviance formulas and the y = 0 branches: correct. NB MoM alpha = (Pearson - df)/sum mu: correct moment equation. NB theta score and Hessian in `mle_nb_alpha`: verified by differentiation.
- IRLS start `ln(y + 0.5)`: standard; `w.max(1e-12)` at glm.rs:743 is inert because mu is clamped to [1e-9, 1-1e-9] first (w >= 1e-9).
- `zsum.max(EPSILON)`: dead guard, not a bug (listed under DRY).
- gam.rs:1053 `(n - edf).max(1e-8)`: only reachable when n <= edf, where it makes GCV enormous and that lambda is rejected; harmless. gam.rs:314 division by `sum`: guarded (`sum > 0`, finite). Quantile knots with heavy ties: zero-width spans are skipped by the span search and the right-boundary fallback; degenerate basis columns make B'B singular and fail closed at lambda = 0.
- `partial_cmp(..).unwrap_or(Equal)` sorts: gam (x validated finite), divergence (inputs validated finite) are safe. m_estimate/matching sorts see NaN only if the backend accepted NaN input; prefer `total_cmp` anyway (Rust >= 1.81 may panic on inconsistent comparators).
- MAD constant 1.4826 = 1/Phi^-1(0.75): correct.
- `gaussian_kl` log handling (divergence.rs:42): variances validated positive/finite; log1p branch correct. `gaussian_segment_lr` logs guarded by the v == 0 branches (see stats-a-3 for what those branches return).
- cox_ipcw: Breslow is what is claimed and what is implemented (deaths*ln(risk), jump = d/risk); censoring is modelled on event == 0; G(t-) uses strictly earlier jumps so events precede censorings at ties; no fallback/regularisation; covariates standardised and coefficients un-scaled correctly.
- interference.rs:340 `_ => Ok(())`: all three `AssignmentDesign` variants have explicit guarded arms; `_` is the "guards did not fire" success path, not a swallowed variant (it would silently accept a future 4th variant — minor). `binary_search(..).expect` at :380/:419 searches ids derived from the same slice; cannot fail. The Young-bound variance algebra (sum Var_i / n; contrast 2(Va+Vb)) is correct and conservative.
- special.rs: `ln_gamma` rel. err <= 1.7e-15 vs `math.lgamma` on 1e-8..1e10 and negative non-integers; `digamma` <= 3e-14; `student_t_ppf` exact to <= 1e-12 vs closed forms for df = 1, 2 incl. p = 1e-10 and 1-1e-6. `normal_ppf` not re-ported (depends on kernels crate) — its in-crate test pins scipy values at 1e-15..1-1e-15, but with tolerance 5e-10, looser than the "near machine epsilon" doc claim.
- fdr.rs BH/BY/Holm/Bonferroni incl. invalid-p handling: correct.

### stats-a — reviewed and sound

- covariance.rs sandwich assembly, HC0-3, one-way cluster, NW, panel HAC meat (time-label lagging, no cross-unit products).
- cluster.rs tuple interning, CGM signs, `few_cluster_t_ratio`.
- glm.rs logistic/probit/Poisson/NB2 IRLS weights and working responses; multinomial score/information blocks; deviances.
- gam.rs Cox–de Boor evaluation and derivative recurrence, D2'D2 penalty, edf = tr((B'B+lambda P)^-1 B'B) - 1 argument, unweighted centred GCV.
- cox_ipcw.rs partial likelihood / score / information / Breslow hazard / line search.
- observation.rs KM-IPCW with delayed entry (G(L-)/G(T-)), AIPW pseudo-values.
- interference.rs exact enumeration, MC sampler, HT/Hajek and conservative variance.
- twosls.rs 2SLS structural residuals and first-stage partial F.
- regularized.rs elastic-net coordinate update and back-transform.
- response.rs local-polynomial kernel sandwich and HC0 influence SE; `robust_local_polynomial` scaling.
- special.rs gamma/beta/t functions (numerically verified).

### stats-ci — refuted leads

- **analytic.rs Fisher z / df.** p-value is the exact Student-t test on t = r·sqrt(df/(1−r²)) with df = n−2−|Z| (correct; not a normal approximation). CI uses Fisher z with SE = 1/sqrt(df−1) = 1/sqrt(n−3−|Z|) (correct). df <= 0 is an error on the analytic path (parcorr.rs:120), CI returns NaN for df<=0 and [−1,1] for df<=1, deliberately and tested. Clamp ±(1−1e-15) keeps 1−r² ≈ 2e-15 > 0; fine.
- **advanced.rs:489 `pxy·ln(..)` zero guard.** Guard exists (`pxy>0 && px>0 && py>0`) and only observed cells are iterated. No NaN.
- **dCor estimator.** Biased V-statistic (Székely 2007) with double centering, dCor = sqrt(dCov²/sqrt(dVarX·dVarY)); consistent with its comment. Because significance is by permutation, the bias cancels; the unbiased U-statistic is not needed. `dcor_from_sides` is differentially tested bit-for-bit against the monolithic form.
- **Permutation p-value.** `(1+#{>=})/(1+B)` with `>=` everywhere (block_shuffle, weighted, multivariate, knn, symbolic, gpdc, g², ppc). Correct.
- **kNN "CMI" digamma / KSG terms.** There are none: the test is explicitly not KSG (doc, factory, provenance all say so). The defect is elsewhere (finding 1).
- **Gamma sampler.** Marsaglia–Tsang (d=a−1/3, c=1/sqrt(9d), squeeze 1−0.0331x⁴, log test 0.5x²+d(1−v+ln v)) is correct for shape >= 1; shape < 1 uses Gamma(a)=Gamma(a+1)·U^{1/a}, correct. `sample_inv_gamma(shape, scale)` passes scale as the Gamma *rate*, which is the right reciprocal relationship.
- **NIG log marginal (bayes.rs:379-433).** Matches the closed form with prior mean 0: −n/2·ln2π + ½(ln|Λ0|−ln|Λn|) + α0 lnβ0 − αn lnβn + lnΓ(αn) − lnΓ(α0), βn = β0 + ½(y'y − mₙ'X'y). Posterior draw β = m + σ L⁻ᵀz is correct.
- **Seed families sharing random numbers inside one analysis.** Each CI test type has its own constant (0xC1, 0x77C1, 0x77C2, 0xC11, 0x51C, 0x69DC, 0x65C0); one analysis uses one CI test, and collisions between families need > 3000 queries in one batch. Discovery's own streams (1000+, 2000+, 3000+ chain) can only collide with 0xC11+qi for qi in a narrow range of a huge batch. Not a practical problem; the real issues are in findings 9 and 10.
- **Bartlett/Wilks multiplier.** `(n − (|Z|+1)) − (px+py+1)/2`, df = px·py: matches Bartlett's approximation with the intercept and Z partialled out.
- **G² df with structural zeros.** df is computed per stratum from the levels observed in that stratum, so empty rows/columns are already removed (the Tigramite/pgmpy convention). The problem is sparsity bias (finding 2) and the df floor (finding 11), not the zero handling.
- **FDR helpers.** Live in `src/fdr.rs`, outside this unit; not reviewed here.

### stats-ci — reviewed and sound

- `analytic_parcorr_pvalue`, `analytic_parcorr_ci`, `z_to_r`.
- `block_permute_contiguous` and the inline block shuffles (permutation validity, short last block, buffer reuse). Unconditional block-shuffle null for ParCorr/kNN/SymbolicCmi/G².
- `residual_canonical`: whitening A = Lx⁻¹ Sxy Ly⁻ᵀ, Gram in the smaller dimension, ln det(I − AAᵀ) = ln Λ, index conventions for both px<=py and px>py branches; `leading_psd_eigenvalue` (power iteration; adequate for a reported statistic).
- `MultivariatePartialCorrelation::test_blocks` analytic and permutation paths (full re-derivation of Λ per replicate, `<=` direction).
- `weighted_pearson` / `weighted_residuals` algebra (WLS normal equations, weighted centering).
- `rank_column` midranks on finite input.
- `g_squared_on_rows` contingency accumulation, expected counts, 2·Σ O ln(O/E); `chi2_sf` via Q(df/2, x/2); within-stratum permutation null.
- `conditional_symbolic_mi` (size-weighted average of stratum MI).
- `gp_residual` algebra (Kα = y_c − λα) given its hyper-parameters.
- `log_marginal_nig`, `draw_m0_replicate`, `logistic_from_log_bf`, PPC p-value construction.
- `knn_input_fingerprint` cache invalidation (full-content hash; tested).
- `uniform_bin_chi2`, `chi2_crit_approx` tabulated values (0.001 upper tail, df 1–10 correct).

### tests-quality — refuted leads

- `test_transport_exact.py:472` / `test_transport_statistical.py:105` "no assertions": the runpy'd examples carry 8 and 7 asserts; effective.
- `test_analysis_workflow.py:200` and `test_compiler_consistency.py:292` either-class raises: both assert on the caught value (`.study`, `.report.operation`, `reason_code in REGISTERED`); adequate.
- "matching coverage never measured": a cell exists (`matching_homoskedastic_ci_coverage`), but only homoskedastic/homogeneous ATT (finding 3/10) — refined, not refuted.
- 72 `importorskip("antecedent")`: low risk. The wheel CI job runs `python -c "import antecedent"` before pytest (ci.yml ~:284), and the source job has `--cov-fail-under=85`, so a mass skip cannot go green. Residual: `importorskip("pyarrow")`/`"pandas"`/`"antecedent._native"` (test_ingest_parity.py:14,173; test_arrow_*; test_result_containers.py:123) could skip silently if an extra is dropped from the CI install line; cheap fix is `-rs` plus `--strict-markers` and a conftest `pytest_terminal_summary` that fails on any skip whose reason is not in an allowlist.
- "pinned PAG fixture is not a valid ancestral graph": the fixtures the sub-audit checked (mag_visible_numeric, temporal_class_envelope/latent.json, pag_ate_envelope, generalized_adjustment) are valid; the gap is missing fixture classes, not invalid ones. (The specific fixture named by the other reviewer was not identified to me; not re-checked.)
- block_length_tests.rs:223,229 flagged as orphans by my script: refuted, matched by the prefix filter at gate_calibration.sh:230.
- No `|rate - nominal| < 0.04`-style override exists anywhere in the calibration harness; no tuned seeds found (SplitMix-scrambled `base + rep`).
- All 463 `evidence_test`/`evidence_assertion` pointers in support_licensed.toml resolve to existing files and fn names.
- Notebook install pin `antecedent>=1.11.0,<1.12` matches the workspace version 1.11.0.

### tests-quality — reviewed and sound

- Calibration harness core: two-sided level +- 3 MCSE, non-finite/inverted interval counted as a miss, per-grid-point verdicts, records bound to facade-reported construction, smoke runs cannot enter the registry; aggregate bias over 1040 non-boundary points is +0.0012 / -0.0010.
- transport_exact_scm.rs:27-122 independent latent enumeration at 1e-12 with negative-witness mutation and `verify_s_hedge` on every non-transportable verdict.
- path_specific_edge_gformula and cpdag_ate_envelope reference scripts (independent numpy, re-runnable).
- temporal_class_envelope_numeric_pins.rs identified-set test: independent in-test OLS per completion plus independently asserted adjustment sets and a non-degeneracy check (> 0.1 width).
- conformance/UNEXERCISED.toml + gate_evidence_reachability.sh: every fixture directory must have an executing consumer (empty list).
- test_notebook_api_surface.py does what it says (static name resolution), and says it does not execute.

### validate — refuted leads

- "Six `panic!` sites in library code": all `panic!` are in `src/tests.rs` (`#[cfg(test)]`). Non-test `expect`s are guarded: `stability/regime.rs:100` (length equality checked at `:72`, indices come from a resample of the same length), `riesz.rs:159` (outcome requested with `true`), `bayesian_checks.rs:277` (all-`Some` checked at `:270`).
- "`graph_refute.rs` CI-tests implied independences / multiple testing": it does not; it is leave-one-out covariate dropping (module doc says so, report id `adjustment.drop_covariate`). See validate-13 for the real issue.
- "`common.rs:146-151` four divisions by `w_sum`": guarded by `w_sum <= 0.0 -> None` at `:143`; NaN weights fail `*w <= 0.0`? — NaN is not `<= 0`, so a NaN weight would enter; but callers pass posterior masses. `PredictiveCheckReport::mixture_weighted` does check finiteness (`:228`); `RefutationReport::mixture_weighted` does not (very low).
- "`(w/max_weight)/total` at `bayesian_checks.rs:258,278,302`": correct overflow-safe normalisation; total-variance SD via `hypot` is right (law of total variance), test at `:1251` verifies √5.
- "`from_seed(seed)` and `from_seed(seed ^ REPLICATE_NOISE_STREAM)` shared streams": within the prior check the β stream and noise stream are distinct; the prior-check noise stream and posterior-check noise stream are the same sequence, but they feed two separately-reported checks and are never combined, so each remains marginally valid. (They do bypass `ctx.rng.stream`, low.)
- "`sorted.last().unwrap_or(1.0)` reports a missing value as maximally robust": unreachable — all four callers reject an empty grid first. The real problem with that line is validate-1.
- "`sensitivity.rs:175-176` SD floor": benign for the grid (a zero residual SD makes the perturbation vanish and the refit singular -> error), noted only under validate-14 for the NaN-swallowing `max`.
- "Refuter that errors on some replicates reports from survivors": false for all refuters except the two block-bootstrap paths (validate-4).
- "Placebo: permutation vs fresh draw, preserves marginal?": both exist; default is Gaussian (does not preserve a binary marginal; harmless for OLS, and `effect_refit` estimators needing 0/1 will error rather than mislead). Permute mode shuffles only complete-case rows and preserves the marginal — correct. Both are subject to validate-2.
- "`reductions -> 0.0` at `bayesian_checks.rs:1112-1113,1159,1179`": `unwrap_or(0.0)` fires only on empty input; `n_sims=0` yields `p = (1+0)/(1+0) = 1` -> passes with zero simulations (very low; reject `n_sims == 0`).
- MC p-value form `(1+count)/(1+n)`, two-sided `2·min(tails)` capped at 1, inclusive tails for ties: correct.

### validate — reviewed and sound

- `assemble_perturbed_normal_eq` / `SensitivityGram` (`sensitivity.rs:283-393`): algebra of X'ᵀX', X'ᵀY' from the Gram of [1,T,Z,u] verified term by term; row_selection mapping of `u` correct.
- Partial-R² calibration `scale = √(r/(1−r))·SD(T|Z)` gives partial R²(T'~u|Z) = r exactly; worst-case sign orientation correct.
- `nw_loo_predict_pair` log-weight shift `exp(−½(d−m)(d+m)/h²)`: equals Gaussian kernel ratio, LOO exclusion correct, denominator never zero.
- `with_contiguous_row_window` (start uniform over span+1, clamp), `with_row_subset`, `with_resampled_rows` masks; permute-mode eligibility logic.
- `coverage_report` percentile indices and fixed-b rescaling about the replicate mean; NaN replicates fail closed there.
- `serial_discrepancy`: realized-discrepancy PPC (Gelman–Meng–Stern) with iid N(0,1) replicated residuals is correct because T is scale-free; tails/p-value correct.
- Posterior-predictive σ² draw `SSR/χ²_n` via `Gamma(n/2, rate ½)`; `sample_poisson` chunked Knuth (exact by additivity) with normal approx above 1e4.
- `PriorSensitivity::to_report` relative range (overflow-safe, unit-invariant), `RefutationReport::mixture_weighted` fail-closed pass rule, `e_value_from_risk_ratio` overflow-safe form, Riesz Cauchy–Schwarz bound |E[αΔ]| ≤ ‖α‖₂‖Δ‖₂, `ConflictPolicy::shrink_alpha` monotone non-increasing.
- `suite.rs` error routing: Cancelled propagates, NotApplicable distinct from Failed, Failed distinct from a failed report.

## Source

Per-unit findings with full failure scenarios, evidence and scratch simulations: `.handover/audit-pre-calibration/findings/<unit>.md` (git-ignored). Machine-readable list: `.handover/audit-pre-calibration/findings.json`.
